LEARNLevel by level

Part 1 · Why OOP (Q41) Part 2 · Building blocks (Q42, Q44) Part 3 · Constructor, static (Q45, Q46) Part 4 · Inheritance, polymorphism (Q47) Part 5 · Interfaces (Q43, Q48)
PART 1 · WHY OOP

🤔 What is Object-Oriented ABAP? (Q41)

Until now (Module 3) we had data in one place (global variables) and code in another (FORMs, FMs). Any FORM could change any global variable.

OOP puts the data and the code that works on it together in one box called a class.

🏗️ Class vs object

  • 📐 Class = the plan (blueprint). Written once. Example: lcl_gr = "how a GR helper works".
  • 🏠 Object = a real thing made from the plan, living in memory while the program runs. Example: one GR helper for plant 1000, another for plant 2000.
  • 🔁 One class → many objects. Each object has its own data.
📐 class lcl_gr→ NEW →🏠 object for plant 1000·🏠 object for plant 2000

❓ Why use it?

🕰️ FORM / FM style🎯 OOP style
DataGlobal, anyone can changeInside the class, can be private
ReuseCopy or call FMCreate objects, inherit, interfaces
Errorssy-subrcExceptions (TRY … CATCH)
TestingHardABAP Unit tests
FutureObsolete (FORM)ABAP Cloud, RAP, BADIs, SALV are all classes

🔤 The 4 pillars you will learn: Encapsulation (L4) · Inheritance (L7) · Polymorphism (L8) · Abstraction (L10).

🧠 Remember: class = plan, object = real thing made with NEW. Data + code in one box.

🔧 Your first class (2 parts)

A local class (inside a report) always has 2 parts: DEFINITION = "what it has" (menu card) and IMPLEMENTATION = "how it works" (kitchen).

CLASS lcl_gr DEFINITION.
  PUBLIC SECTION.
    METHODS get_total IMPORTING iv_ebeln TYPE ebeln
                      RETURNING VALUE(rv_total) TYPE menge_d.
ENDCLASS.

CLASS lcl_gr IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD get_total.
    SELECT SUM( menge ) FROM mseg
      WHERE ebeln = @iv_ebeln AND bwart = '101' INTO @rv_total.
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

📞 Use it

START-OF-SELECTION.
  DATA(lo_gr)    = NEW lcl_gr( ).               " make an object
  DATA(lv_total) = lo_gr->get_total( p_ebeln ).  " call its method
  WRITE: / 'GR total:', lv_total.

🚶 Step by step

  1. lcl_ = local class (lives in this report). Global classes start with ZCL_ and live in SE24.
  2. METHODS = like a FORM, but inside the class. IMPORTING = input. RETURNING VALUE(rv_total) = one result sent back.
  3. NEW lcl_gr( ) creates the object in memory. lo_gr holds a reference (address) to it. lo_ = local object.
  4. lo_gr->get_total( … ) = "object lo_gr, run your get_total". -> = arrow to the object.
🔁 FORM (Module 3)🎯 Method
FORM … ENDFORMMETHOD … ENDMETHOD inside a class
PERFORM get_total USING … CHANGING …lv = lo_gr->get_total( … )
USING / CHANGINGIMPORTING / EXPORTING / CHANGING / RETURNING

🕰️ Old syntax you'll see: CREATE OBJECT lo_gr. = same as NEW. CALL METHOD lo_gr->get_total EXPORTING … = same call, long form.

🧠 Remember: DEFINITION = menu, IMPLEMENTATION = kitchen. NEW makes it, -> calls it.
PART 2 · BUILDING BLOCKS

🧱 What is inside a class? (Q42)

ComponentKeywordSimple meaningGR example
📦 AttributeDATAData the object remembersmv_werks plant
⚙️ MethodMETHODSCode the object can runget_total( )
🔔 EventEVENTS"Something happened" signal others can listen togr_posted
🔒 ConstantCONSTANTSFixed valuec_bwart = '101'
🏷️ TypesTYPESStructures used by the classty_gr
🐣 ConstructorMETHODS constructorRuns automatically at NEW (L5)set plant

Also: sections (who can see what, L4), interfaces (L9) and friends (a class allowed to see your private parts, rare).

🔤 Name style

mv_ member variable (attribute) · iv_ importing · ev_ exporting · rv_ returning · lo_ object reference · lcl_ local class · lif_ local interface · ZCL_ / ZIF_ global.

🏠 Local vs global class

Local classGlobal class
WhereInside one report (SE38)SE24 / ADT, own object
Namelcl_grZCL_GR_HELPER
Who can useThat report onlyWhole system
LikeFORMFunction module
🧠 Remember: class = attributes (data) + methods (code) + events. Local = one report, global = SE24.

🔒 Public, Protected, Private (Q44)

Each class has up to 3 sections. They decide who can see and use each attribute or method.

SectionWho can use itUse for
🌍 PUBLICEveryoneThe "buttons" others may press
👨‍👧 PROTECTEDThe class + its subclasses (children, L7)Things children need
🔐 PRIVATEOnly the class itselfIts own data and helper methods
CLASS lcl_gr_item DEFINITION.
  PUBLIC SECTION.
    METHODS: set_qty IMPORTING iv_qty TYPE menge_d,
             get_qty RETURNING VALUE(rv_qty) TYPE menge_d.
  PRIVATE SECTION.
    DATA mv_qty TYPE menge_d.        " nobody outside can touch
ENDCLASS.
CLASS lcl_gr_item IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD set_qty.
    IF iv_qty < 0. RETURN. ENDIF.   " guard: no negative GR qty
    mv_qty = iv_qty.
  ENDMETHOD.
  METHOD get_qty. rv_qty = mv_qty. ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

🚶 Why this is good

  1. Outside code writes lo_item->mv_qty = -5. → syntax error. Private!
  2. It must use lo_item->set_qty( -5 ). The method checks and refuses.
  3. So the quantity can never become wrong. This is encapsulation: hide the data, open only safe doors.
🪤 Traps: sections must be written in the order PUBLIC → PROTECTED → PRIVATE. Private is not visible to subclasses (use PROTECTED for that).
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q42, Q44)

"A class has attributes, methods, events, constants and types, plus a constructor. Visibility sections control access: public for everyone, protected for the class and its subclasses, private only for the class itself. In my GR helper class the quantity was a private attribute and could only be set through a public method that rejected negative values, which is encapsulation. Once a subclass couldn't read a private attribute, so I moved it to the protected section."

🧠 Remember: Public = park, Protected = family, Private = bedroom.
PART 3 · CONSTRUCTOR, STATIC

🐣 What is a constructor? (Q45)

A constructor is a special method that SAP runs automatically when an object is created with NEW. Use it to set up the object: fill starting values, check input.

CLASS lcl_gr DEFINITION.
  PUBLIC SECTION.
    METHODS constructor IMPORTING iv_werks TYPE werks_d.
  PRIVATE SECTION.
    DATA mv_werks TYPE werks_d.
ENDCLASS.
CLASS lcl_gr IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD constructor.
    mv_werks = iv_werks.      " runs by itself at NEW
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

DATA(lo_gr) = NEW lcl_gr( iv_werks = '1000' ).

🔑 WERKS_D = data element for plant. NEW lcl_gr( iv_werks = '1000' ) = create the object and pass the plant to the constructor in one go.

📏 Rules

  • 📛 Name must be exactly constructor.
  • ➡️ Only IMPORTING parameters. No EXPORTING, no RETURNING.
  • 🔁 Runs once per object. You can't call it yourself like a normal method.
  • 👨‍👧 In a subclass, the constructor must first call super->constructor( ).
🐣 CONSTRUCTOR🏛️ CLASS_CONSTRUCTOR
TypeInstanceStatic
RunsEach time an object is madeOnce, before the class is first used
ParametersIMPORTING onlyNone
UseSet this object's dataLoad shared settings once (e.g. read config table)
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q45)

"A constructor is a special instance method named CONSTRUCTOR that runs automatically when an object is created with NEW or CREATE OBJECT. It can only have importing parameters and runs once per object; CLASS_CONSTRUCTOR is the static version that runs once before the class is first used. My GR class took the plant in its constructor and stored it privately, so every object knew its plant from birth. In a subclass I once forgot super->constructor( ) and got a syntax error, so now I always call it first."

🧠 Remember: constructor = birth certificate, made automatically at NEW. Importing only.

🏛️ Static vs instance (Q46)

  • 🏠 Instance attribute/method = belongs to each object. Need an object first. Call with ->.
  • 🏛️ Static attribute/method = belongs to the class itself, shared by all objects. No object needed. Call with =>. Keywords CLASS-DATA, CLASS-METHODS.
CLASS lcl_gr DEFINITION.
  PUBLIC SECTION.
    CLASS-DATA    gv_count TYPE i.            " ONE for all objects
    DATA          mv_ebeln TYPE ebeln.        " one PER object
    CLASS-METHODS create RETURNING VALUE(ro_gr) TYPE REF TO lcl_gr.
ENDCLASS.
CLASS lcl_gr IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD create.
    ro_gr = NEW #( ).  gv_count = gv_count + 1.
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

DATA(lo1) = lcl_gr=>create( ).   " => static: no object needed
lo1->mv_ebeln = '4500001234'.     " -> instance

🚶 Step by step

  1. lcl_gr=>create( ) runs without any object. It makes one and counts it.
  2. Call it 3 times → 3 objects, each with its own mv_ebeln, but one gv_count = 3.
  3. NEW #( ) = "new object of the type on the left" (here lcl_gr).
🏠 Instance🏛️ Static
KeywordDATA, METHODSCLASS-DATA, CLASS-METHODS
Belongs toEach objectThe class (shared)
Calllo->m( )lcl=>m( )
Needs objectYesNo
Can useInstance + static membersOnly static members (no me)
💡 You already used one! cl_salv_table=>factory( ) in Module 4 = static "create" method.
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q46)

"Instance attributes and methods belong to each object, so I need an object and call them with the arrow. Static ones, declared with CLASS-DATA and CLASS-METHODS, belong to the class, are shared by all objects and are called with the fat arrow without creating an object. A static method can't use instance attributes. I used a static factory method to create GR helper objects and a static counter, like cl_salv_table=>factory in ALV."

🧠 Remember: -> = object (instance), => = class (static, shared).
PART 4 · INHERITANCE

👨‍👦 What is inheritance? (Q47)

A new class (child / subclass) gets everything public and protected from an existing class (parent / superclass), and can add or change things.

  • ❓ Why? GR docs, invoice docs, delivery docs all have a number, a date and "show". Write that once in a parent lcl_doc.
  • 🕰️ Before: copy the same code into 3 FORMs.
CLASS lcl_doc DEFINITION.
  PUBLIC SECTION. METHODS show.
ENDCLASS.
CLASS lcl_doc IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD show. WRITE / 'Document'. ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

CLASS lcl_gr_doc DEFINITION INHERITING FROM lcl_doc.
  PUBLIC SECTION. METHODS show REDEFINITION.
ENDCLASS.
CLASS lcl_gr_doc IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD show. super->show( ). WRITE '· GR 101'. ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

🚶 Step by step

  1. INHERITING FROM lcl_doc = lcl_gr_doc is a child of lcl_doc.
  2. METHODS show REDEFINITION = "I will write my own version of show".
  3. super->show( ) = first run the parent's version ("Document"), then add "· GR 101".
  4. Output for a GR doc: Document · GR 101.
KeywordMeaning
REDEFINITIONChild overrides a parent method (same name, same parameters)
super->Call the parent's version
FINALNo children allowed (class) / can't be redefined (method)
ABSTRACTCan't create objects; only children can (L10)
🚨 PDF fix: ABAP has single inheritance only: one parent per class. For "many parents" use interfaces (L9).
🧠 Remember: INHERITING FROM = one papa. REDEFINITION = my own version. super-> = papa's version.

🎭 What is polymorphism? (Q47)

Poly = many, morph = forms. Same call, different behaviour, depending on the real object behind the reference.

DATA lt_docs TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF REF TO lcl_doc WITH EMPTY KEY.
APPEND NEW lcl_gr_doc( )  TO lt_docs.   " GR (MIGO 101)
APPEND NEW lcl_inv_doc( ) TO lt_docs.   " invoice (MIRO)

LOOP AT lt_docs INTO DATA(lo_doc).
  lo_doc->show( ).   " GR version, then invoice version
ENDLOOP.

🚶 Step by step

  1. lt_docs holds references of type parent (lcl_doc).
  2. We put a GR child and an invoice child into it. Allowed: a child is a doc (this is an up-cast, automatic).
  3. The loop calls show( ) the same way each time.
  4. SAP looks at the real object: GR → GR's show, invoice → invoice's show. The loop never needs IF / CASE.

🆕 New doc type tomorrow (delivery)? Add a child class. The loop code doesn't change at all.

🔁 Casting

  • ⬆️ Up-cast (child → parent variable): automatic, always safe.
  • ⬇️ Down-cast (parent → child variable): lo_gr = CAST #( lo_doc ). (old: ?=). If it isn't really a GR → CX_SY_MOVE_CAST_ERROR. Wrap in TRY.
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q47)

"Inheritance lets a subclass reuse the public and protected parts of a superclass using INHERITING FROM; ABAP allows only one superclass, and multiple behaviour comes from interfaces. A subclass can redefine a method and call super-> for the parent version. Polymorphism means the same method call behaves differently depending on the real object. In my project a superclass lcl_doc had show, and GR and invoice subclasses redefined it; one loop over parent references printed both correctly. A down-cast once failed with CX_SY_MOVE_CAST_ERROR, so I wrapped it in TRY CATCH."

🧠 Remember: same button, different TV. Parent reference, child behaviour.
PART 5 · INTERFACES

📜 What is an interface? (Q48)

An interface is a list of method names with no code. A promise. Any class that "signs" it must write the code for every method.

  • ❓ Why? GR doc and a vendor are not parent/child, but both must be "printable". The interface says: "if you're printable, you have print".
  • ➕ A class can implement many interfaces (but has only one parent).
INTERFACE lif_printable.
  METHODS print.
ENDINTERFACE.

CLASS lcl_gr_doc DEFINITION.
  PUBLIC SECTION.
    INTERFACES lif_printable.     " ✅ in DEFINITION
ENDCLASS.
CLASS lcl_gr_doc IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD lif_printable~print.    " interface~method
    WRITE / 'GR slip for MIGO 101'.
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

🚶 Step by step

  1. INTERFACE … ENDINTERFACE: only the method name and parameters.
  2. INTERFACES lif_printable goes in the PUBLIC SECTION of the DEFINITION.
  3. In IMPLEMENTATION, write METHOD lif_printable~print. The ~ joins interface name and method name.
  4. Use it: DATA lo_p TYPE REF TO lif_printable. lo_p = NEW lcl_gr_doc( ). lo_p->print( ).
💡 You already did this in BTP! IF_OO_ADT_CLASSRUN is an interface. That's why you write METHOD if_oo_adt_classrun~main. And every BADI (Module 7) is an interface you implement.
🚨 PDF fix: INTERFACES goes in the DEFINITION, not in the IMPLEMENTATION.
🧠 Remember: interface = promise list, no code. INTERFACES in DEFINITION, METHOD intf~meth in IMPLEMENTATION.

🆚 Class vs Interface (Q43)

🏛️ Class📜 Interface
Has code?Yes (IMPLEMENTATION)No, only declarations
Create object?Yes (unless ABSTRACT)Never
How many?One parent classMany interfaces per class
VisibilityPublic / protected / privateAll components public
Keyword to useINHERITING FROMINTERFACES
Relationship"is a" (GR doc is a doc)"can do" (GR doc can print)
Global T-codeSE24 (ZCL_)SE24 (ZIF_)

🧊 Abstract class: in the middle

CLASS lcl_doc DEFINITION ABSTRACT. = no objects of it. It can have some code and some ABSTRACT methods (no code) that children must write. Use it when children share code and must fill gaps. This is abstraction: show what, hide how.

🎤 INTERVIEW (Q43, Q48)

"A class has attributes and implemented methods and can create objects; an interface only declares methods, has no implementation and can't be instantiated. A class inherits from one superclass but can implement many interfaces, declared with INTERFACES in the public section of the definition, and each method is implemented as interface~method. I created an interface for printable documents implemented by the GR and invoice classes, and in BTP every runnable class implements IF_OO_ADT_CLASSRUN~MAIN. BADIs work the same way."

🧠 Remember: class = "is a", one parent. Interface = "can do", many.

😂 OOP tricks

  • 📐🏠 Class = naksha, Object = ghar. One naksha, many ghar. NEW = build the house.
  • 🍽️ DEFINITION = menu card, IMPLEMENTATION = kitchen. Customer sees menu, not kitchen.
  • 🌳 Public = park, Protected = family, Private = bedroom.
  • ➡️ -> thin arrow = one object. ⏩ => fat arrow = whole class ("double road for the whole colony").
  • 🐣 Constructor = birth certificate: made automatically at birth (NEW), only takes info in.
  • 👨‍👦 Inheritance = one papa only in ABAP. Interfaces = many certificates (you can have many).
  • 📺 Polymorphism = same remote button, different TV.
  • 〰️ Tilde ~ = "ka": lif_printable~print = "printable ka print".
  • 🔤 4 pillars "EIPA": Encapsulation, Inheritance, Polymorphism, Abstraction → "Ek Idli, Pav, Aloo" 🍽️.

🎮 Your turn (answer first, then tap)

🎤 FULL MODULE ANSWER

"Object-oriented ABAP puts data and logic into classes; objects are created from them with NEW. A class has attributes, methods, events and a constructor, with public, protected and private sections for encapsulation. Static members belong to the class and are called with =>, instance members with ->. ABAP supports single inheritance with method redefinition, polymorphism through superclass references, and interfaces for shared behaviour. In my GR work I wrapped the MSEG logic in a class with a private quantity and a plant constructor, and used a superclass for GR and invoice documents. In BTP every class I run implements IF_OO_ADT_CLASSRUN."

DOHands-on

✅ BTP is perfect for OOP. No WRITE there: make methods return a string and print with out->write( ).

  1. ADT: new class ZCL_OOP_DEMO with interface IF_OO_ADT_CLASSRUN. Notice METHOD if_oo_adt_classrun~main = an interface method (L9)!
  2. Tab Local Types: write lcl_doc with show RETURNING VALUE(rv) TYPE string, and child lcl_gr_doc with REDEFINITION.
  3. In main: table of REF TO lcl_doc, append a doc and a GR doc, loop, out->write( lo_doc->show( ) ). F9 → two different texts (polymorphism).
  4. Add a private attribute + constructor (L4, L5). Try to set it from main and read the syntax error.
  1. SE38: report with the L2 local class lcl_gr. Run, then debug: F5 into the method, see lo_gr as an object.
  2. SE24: global class ZCL_GR_HELPER with static method GET_TOTAL. Test it with F8 in SE24.
  3. Call zcl_gr_helper=>get_total( p_ebeln ) from your GR report.
  4. Add the L7 inheritance and L9 interface code to a test report. Use Pattern → ABAP Objects to insert method calls.

TESTQuick check

1. Who can use a PROTECTED attribute?

Public = everyone, protected = class + children, private = class only.

2. A constructor can have…

CONSTRUCTOR takes IMPORTING parameters only. CLASS_CONSTRUCTOR takes none.

3. How do you call a static method?

Fat arrow => on the class name, no object needed.

4. How many superclasses can an ABAP class have?

Single inheritance. Many interfaces are allowed.

5. Where do you write INTERFACES lif_printable?

Declare in DEFINITION, implement as METHOD lif_printable~print.

6. One loop calls lo_doc->show( ); GR and invoice objects print differently. This is…

Same call, different behaviour by the real object.
Answer all 6 first.
⚡ Quick revise. Read top to bottom in 2 minutes. Forgot something? Switch to 📖 Learn.

📐 Part 1 · Class & object (Q41)

  • Class = plan (DEFINITION = menu, IMPLEMENTATION = kitchen). Object = real thing from NEW.
  • DATA(lo) = NEW lcl_gr( ). · lv = lo->get_total( p_ebeln ). · old: CREATE OBJECT, CALL METHOD.
  • Why: data + code together, private data, exceptions, unit tests, ABAP Cloud / RAP / BADI / SALV.
  • 4 pillars "Ek Idli Pav Aloo": Encapsulation, Inheritance, Polymorphism, Abstraction.

🧱 Part 2 · Components & visibility (Q42, Q44)

  • Attributes (DATA), methods, events, constants, types, constructor.
  • Public = park (all) · Protected = family (class + subclasses) · Private = bedroom (class only). Order: PUBLIC → PROTECTED → PRIVATE.
  • Local lcl_ in one report · global ZCL_ in SE24.

🐣 Part 3 · Constructor & static (Q45, Q46)

  • CONSTRUCTOR: auto at NEW, once per object, IMPORTING only. Subclass → super->constructor( ) first.
  • CLASS_CONSTRUCTOR: static, once before first use, no params.
  • Instance (DATA / METHODS, ->, per object) vs static (CLASS-DATA / CLASS-METHODS, =>, shared, no me). E.g. cl_salv_table=>factory( ).

👨‍👦 Part 4 · Inheritance & polymorphism (Q47)

  • INHERITING FROM (one parent only) · REDEFINITION · super->m( ) · FINAL · ABSTRACT.
  • Polymorphism: parent reference, child behaviour. Loop over REF TO lcl_doc → GR / invoice show.
  • Up-cast automatic · down-cast CAST #( ) / ?= → may raise CX_SY_MOVE_CAST_ERROR.

📜 Part 5 · Interfaces (Q43, Q48)

INTERFACES lif_printable.      " PUBLIC SECTION of DEFINITION
METHOD lif_printable~print.     " in IMPLEMENTATION
  • Interface: no code, no objects, all public, many per class, "can do".
  • Class: code, objects, one parent, "is a". Abstract class = in between.
  • IF_OO_ADT_CLASSRUN~MAIN and BADIs = interfaces.
🪤 Traps / PDF fixes: multiple inheritance? No. INTERFACES in IMPLEMENTATION? No, DEFINITION. Private visible to subclass? No. Static method uses instance data? No.
🎤 30-SECOND ANSWER

"OO ABAP puts data and logic into classes, and objects are created with NEW. Classes have attributes, methods, events and a constructor, with public, protected and private sections. Static members use =>, instance members ->. ABAP has single inheritance with redefinition, polymorphism through superclass references, and interfaces for shared behaviour, declared in the definition and implemented as interface~method. In BTP every runnable class implements IF_OO_ADT_CLASSRUN."

🔌
MODULE 07 · PDF Q19, Q49–53, Q69 · COMPLETE ✅

Enhancements & BADI

Add your own logic to SAP standard (like MIGO) without changing SAP's code. T-codes: SMOD, CMOD, SE18, SE19, SE20, FIBF.

😫 PROBLEMBusiness: "Block a 101 GR in MIGO if the storage location is empty." MIGO is SAP's code. You can't just edit it.
💡 IDEASAP leaves "sockets" in its code. You plug your logic in. SAP code stays untouched, upgrades stay safe.
🔧 HOWUser exits → customer exits (CMOD) → BADIs (SE18/SE19) → enhancement framework (implicit/explicit), BTE for FI.
➡️ NEXTModule 8: BDC (load data through screens).

LEARNLevel by level

Part 1 · Why enhance (Q19, Q51) Part 2 · Exits (Q49) Part 3 · BADI (Q19) Part 4 · Enhancement Framework (Q50, Q51) Part 5 · BTE, finding exits (Q52, Q53) Part 6 · Scenario (Q69)
PART 1 · WHY ENHANCE

🤔 What is an enhancement?

SAP gives standard programs: MIGO (GR), ME21N (PO), VA01 (sales order). Every company wants a few extra rules in them.

An enhancement = your own code added at a place SAP prepared for it. SAP's code is not touched.

🆚 Modification vs enhancement

🔨 Modification🔌 Enhancement
WhatChange SAP's own codeAdd code at an SAP "socket"
NeedsAccess key from SAP (SSCR)Nothing special
UpgradeMust be checked again (SPAU), may be lostStays safe
SupportSAP may refuse helpSupported
UseLast option onlyAlways first
📦
Our GR story for this module: "In MIGO, for movement type 101, don't allow posting if the storage location (LGORT, Lagerort) is empty, and write every 101 GR to our log table ZGR_LOG."
🧠 Remember: never touch SAP code. Plug into SAP's sockets.

🔌 The 5 kinds of sockets (oldest → newest)

#TechniqueWhere you workIn simple words
1👴 User exitSAP includes, e.g. MV45AFZZEmpty FORMs USEREXIT_… inside SAP code (mainly SD). Needs access key
2🧓 Customer exitSMOD / CMODSAP calls a function EXIT_…; you fill its Z include
3🧑 Classic BADISE18 / SE19An interface: you write a class (OOP, Module 6)
4🧒 Enhancement FrameworkSE20, Enhance buttonNew BADIs + implicit / explicit points almost anywhere
5💰 BTEFIBFEvents mainly in FI postings

🧩 What is the Enhancement Framework? (Q51)

Since NetWeaver 7.0 (2006) SAP put all modern enhancement types under one roof, managed in SE20:

  • 🔹 Implicit enhancement points: at the start/end of every FORM, FM, method, include. Ready everywhere, no SAP preparation.
  • 🔸 Explicit points / sections: placed by SAP on purpose (ENHANCEMENT-POINT, ENHANCEMENT-SECTION).
  • 🧠 New (kernel) BADIs: GET BADI / CALL BADI, inside enhancement spots.
  • ➕ Class and FM enhancements: add parameters or methods to SAP objects.

📦 Enhancement spot = SAP's container of sockets. Enhancement implementation = your container of plugs.

☁️ S/4HANA today: key users add fields and simple logic in Fiori apps (Custom Fields, Custom Logic). Developers use released BADIs. Big extras go side-by-side on BTP.
🧠 Remember: User exit → Customer exit → BADI → Enhancement Framework. BTE for FI.
PART 2 · EXITS

👴 User exit

SAP left empty FORMs inside its own includes. You write code inside them. Classic example: sales order.

" include MV45AFZZ (SAP's, sales order VA01)
FORM userexit_save_document_prepare.
  " your check before the order is saved
ENDFORM.
  • 📍 Names start with USEREXIT_. Includes like MV45AFZZ (sales), RV60AFZZ (billing).
  • 🔑 Technically a modification: you need an access key. Mostly in SD.

🧓 Customer exit (function exit)

SAP's program contains CALL CUSTOMER-FUNCTION '001'. That calls an SAP function module EXIT_<program>_001. Inside it there is INCLUDE ZX…, an empty Z include for you.

SAP program→CALL CUSTOMER-FUNCTION '001'→FM EXIT_SAPMM07M_001→INCLUDE ZX… (your code)

🛠️ Steps

  1. SMOD: find the SAP enhancement (a package of exits), see its components.
  2. CMOD: create your project, e.g. ZGR_PRJ, and assign the enhancement.
  3. Components → double-click the EXIT_… FM → double-click INCLUDE ZX… → create → write code.
  4. Back in CMOD: Activate the project. Not active = your code never runs!

Kinds of customer exits: function exits (code), screen exits (add fields on an SAP screen subscreen), menu exits (add menu entries).

🚨 PDF fix: EXIT_SAPMM06E_001 is a customer exit (SMOD/CMOD), not a user exit. User exits are the USEREXIT_* FORMs.
🧠 Remember: user exit = USEREXIT_ FORM (access key). Customer exit = SMOD find, CMOD build, activate project.

🆚 User exit vs customer exit vs BADI (Q49)

👴 User exit🧓 Customer exit🧑 BADI
TechniqueFORM in SAP includeFunction module + Z includeInterface + your class (OOP)
T-codesSE38 (access key)SMOD, CMODSE18, SE19
Modification?Yes, technicallyNoNo
ImplementationsOneOne per exitMany (if "multiple use")
Filter (e.g. per country)NoNoYes
Data accessAll global data of SAP programOnly FM parametersOnly method parameters
AreaMostly SDAll modules (older)All modules (newer)
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q19, Q49)

"User exits are empty USEREXIT FORMs in SAP includes like MV45AFZZ; changing them is technically a modification. Customer exits are function modules EXIT_program_nnn called with CALL CUSTOMER-FUNCTION, found in SMOD and implemented in a CMOD project through a Z include. BADIs are object-oriented: SAP defines an interface in SE18 and I implement it in a class in SE19; they can have several implementations and filters. For MIGO I used a BADI to block 101 GRs without a storage location. Once my customer exit code didn't run because the CMOD project wasn't active."

🧠 Remember: FORM → FM → class. Only BADI has many implementations + filters.
PART 3 · BADI

🤔 What is a BADI? (Q19)

BADI = Business Add-In. SAP defines an interface (list of methods, no code). SAP's program calls those methods at fixed moments. You write a class that implements the interface. Exactly Module 6 L9!

SAP: BADI definition (SE18)→interface IF_EX_…→You: implementation (SE19)→class ZCL_IM_… with your code

📦 GR example: MB_DOCUMENT_BADI

SAP calls this BADI when a material document (MIGO GR) is saved.

METHOD if_ex_mb_document_badi~mb_document_before_update.
  LOOP AT xmseg INTO DATA(ls_mseg) WHERE bwart = '101'.
    INSERT zgr_log FROM @( VALUE #( mblnr = ls_mseg-mblnr
                                  mjahr = ls_mseg-mjahr
                                  zeile = ls_mseg-zeile ) ).
  ENDLOOP.
ENDMETHOD.

🔑 if_ex_mb_document_badi~… = "interface ka method" (tilde, Module 6). XMSEG = table of the GR items being saved. We log every 101 line in ZGR_LOG. Exact parameters: SE18 → BADI → Interface tab → double-click the method.

✋ For checks while the user is still in MIGO (our "block if storage location empty"), use MB_MIGO_BADI method CHECK_ITEM and return an error message there.

🛠️ Steps (SE18 / SE19)

  1. SE18: open the BADI. Read its documentation, interface and methods. Check multiple use and filter.
  2. SE19: create an implementation ZIM_GR_LOG for it. SAP creates class ZCL_IM_GR_LOG.
  3. Double-click the method → write your code → save, activate the class.
  4. Activate the implementation in SE19. Test in MIGO with a breakpoint in your method.
🧠 Remember: BADI = SAP interface (SE18) + your class (SE19). Activate both.

🆚 Classic BADI vs new (kernel) BADI

🧑 Classic BADI🧒 New BADI
SinceR/3 4.6NetWeaver 7.0 (Enhancement Framework)
Lives inAloneAn enhancement spot (SE20)
SAP calls it withCL_EXITHANDLER=>GET_INSTANCE + method callGET BADI + CALL BADI
SpeedSlower (ABAP lookup)Faster (kernel)
Fallback classNoYes: default code if no implementation
T-codesSE18 / SE19SE18 / SE19 too, plus SE20
" how SAP (or you) calls a NEW BADI
DATA lo_badi TYPE REF TO zbadi_gr_check.
GET BADI lo_badi.
CALL BADI lo_badi->check_item EXPORTING is_mseg = ls_mseg.

🔑 You can also create your own BADI in your Z programs, so other teams can plug in later without changing your code.

🪤 Traps: 1) "Multiple use" off → only one active implementation allowed. 2) With many implementations, the order is not guaranteed. 3) Filter BADI: implementation runs only for its filter value (e.g. plant 1000).
🧠 Remember: classic = CL_EXITHANDLER. New = GET BADI / CALL BADI, faster, in a spot.
PART 4 · ENHANCEMENT FRAMEWORK

🔹 Implicit enhancement (Q50)

SAP has invisible sockets at the start and end of every FORM, function module and method, at the end of includes, and in structure definitions. You can switch them on and add code, without SAP planning it.

🛠️ Steps

  1. Open the SAP source (SE37 / SE38 / SE24) in display mode.
  2. Click Enhance (spiral icon, Shift+F4).
  3. Menu Edit → Enhancement Operations → Show Implicit Enhancement Options. Yellow "″″″″" lines appear.
  4. Right-click a line → Enhancement Implementation → Create Implementation → type Code → name ZEI_GR_CHECK + short text.
  5. Write code between the markers → save → activate.
ENHANCEMENT 1  zei_gr_check.     " added by you, SAP code untouched
  IF ls_mseg-bwart = '101' AND ls_mseg-lgort IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE 'Storage location is required for GR' TYPE 'E'.
  ENDIF.
ENDENHANCEMENT.

🔸 Explicit enhancement

  • ENHANCEMENT-POINT: SAP marked "you may add code here".
  • ENHANCEMENT-SECTION … END-ENHANCEMENT-SECTION: SAP marked "you may replace this block". Your version runs instead of SAP's.
⚠️ Careful: an implicit enhancement at the start of an FM runs for every program that calls it. Check with IF (T-code, movement type) and keep it small. Prefer a BADI if one exists.
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q50, Q51)

"The Enhancement Framework, managed in SE20, groups explicit enhancement points and sections, implicit enhancement points at the start and end of FORMs, FMs and methods, and new BADIs in enhancement spots. For an implicit enhancement I open the source, press Enhance, use Edit, Enhancement Operations, Show Implicit Enhancement Options, create a code implementation and activate it. I used one to check the storage location for 101 GRs when no BADI fitted. Because that FM was called from several programs, I limited the code with an IF on movement type and transaction."

🧠 Remember: implicit = start/end of everything, no SAP prep. Explicit = SAP's point (add) or section (replace).
PART 5 · BTE, FINDING

💰 What is a BTE? (Q52)

BTE = Business Transaction Event. Mainly in FI (also some SD / CO). At fixed moments (e.g. FI document posting) SAP checks a table: "has a customer registered a function module for this event?" If yes, it calls it.

TypeWhat it can do
📢 Publish & Subscribe (P/S)SAP tells you "this happened". You react (log, check). You can't send data back
⚙️ Process interfaceYou replace SAP's standard logic and return data

🛠️ Steps

  1. FIBF → Environment → Info system (P/S) → find the event (e.g. 00001030 = FI document posting).
  2. Copy the sample FM SAMPLE_INTERFACE_00001030 to your Z FM in SE37 (same parameters), write code.
  3. FIBF → Settings → Products → of a customer → create a product, tick active.
  4. FIBF → Settings → P/S modules → of a customer → event + product + your FM.
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q52)

"A Business Transaction Event is an FI-style enhancement: SAP calls registered function modules at fixed events. Publish and subscribe BTEs notify you without returning data; process BTEs replace standard logic. I find the event in FIBF, copy the SAMPLE_INTERFACE function module to a Z FM, then register it with an active customer product in FIBF. My FM didn't run once because the product wasn't marked active."

🧠 Remember: BTE = FIBF, copy SAMPLE_INTERFACE_*, register with an ACTIVE product.

🔎 How to find exits for a T-code (Q53)

Example: "Which sockets does MIGO have?"

  1. 🧭 SE93 → MIGO → note its program and package (System → Status also shows the program).
  2. 🧓 Customer exits: open the program, search (Ctrl+F, "in main program") for CALL CUSTOMER-FUNCTION. Or SMOD → F4 / Info system → search by package.
  3. 🧑 Classic BADIs: breakpoint in method CL_EXITHANDLER=>GET_INSTANCE → run MIGO → each stop shows a BADI name in exit_name.
  4. 🧒 New BADIs: in the debugger set a breakpoint at statement GET BADI (Breakpoints → Breakpoint at → Statement). Or search the program for GET BADI.
  5. 📚 SPRO: many IMG areas have a "Business Add-Ins (BAdIs)" node with documentation.
  6. 🔹 Implicit points: Enhance → Show Implicit Enhancement Options (L7).
📦 For MIGO you'll find: BADIs MB_MIGO_BADI (checks, extra screen) and MB_DOCUMENT_BADI (at save). Pick the one called at the right moment for your rule.
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q53)

"I start with SE93 to get the program and package. For customer exits I search the program for CALL CUSTOMER-FUNCTION or search SMOD by package. For classic BADIs I set a breakpoint in CL_EXITHANDLER=>GET_INSTANCE and run the transaction; for new BADIs I use a statement breakpoint on GET BADI. I also check the BAdI nodes in SPRO. For MIGO this showed MB_MIGO_BADI and MB_DOCUMENT_BADI."

🧠 Remember: SE93 → CALL CUSTOMER-FUNCTION → CL_EXITHANDLER breakpoint → GET BADI breakpoint → SPRO.
PART 6 · SCENARIO

🎭 "Enhance a standard transaction. Your approach?" (Q69)

  1. 📝 Understand the rule exactly (which T-code, mvt type, plant, error or warning?).
  2. ⚙️ Config first: ask the functional consultant. Can SPRO do it (field status, message control)? No code is best code.
  3. 🧭 Find sockets (L9): SE93 → program → BADIs, customer exits, BTE (FI), explicit points.
  4. 🥇 Choose in order: new BADI → classic BADI → customer exit → BTE → explicit enhancement → implicit enhancement → (very last) modification with access key.
  5. 🧪 Build & test in DEV: breakpoint, positive and negative cases, other T-codes that call the same code.
  6. 📦 Document & transport: one transport, clear name (ZIM_…, ZEI_…), tech spec updated. Then QA → PRD (Module 12).
📦
Example: rule "no 101 GR without storage location" → config (field status mandatory) was not possible for one plant only → found MB_MIGO_BADI → filter-free implementation with an IF on plant + mvt type 101 → error message in CHECK_ITEM → tested MIGO 101, 102, 201 → transported.
☁️ On S/4HANA add: check key user extensibility (Custom Fields / Custom Logic apps) and released BADIs first. These survive upgrades best and work in the cloud.
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q69)

"First I clarify the requirement and check with the functional team whether configuration can do it. If code is needed, I find the program in SE93 and look for BADIs, customer exits, BTEs and explicit points, preferring new BADIs, then classic BADIs and customer exits, and implicit enhancements only when nothing else fits; modifications are the last resort. For a rule blocking 101 GRs without a storage location I used MB_MIGO_BADI CHECK_ITEM, tested 101 and other movement types, documented it and transported it. In S/4 I'd also check released BADIs and key user extensibility."

🧠 Remember: config → find → BADI first → implicit late → modification last → test → transport.

😂 Enhancement tricks

  • 🔌 Socket rule: "SAP ki deewar mat todo, socket mein plug lagao" (don't break SAP's wall, use the socket).
  • 👴🧓🧑🧒 Family age order: Dada = user exit, Papa = customer exit, Beta = classic BADI, Pota = new BADI.
  • 🔍 SMOD = Search MODification (look), CMOD = Create MODification project (build). S before C.
  • 🔢 SE18 = 18 years, adult = definition (who decides). SE19 = next year, implementation (you do the work).
  • 🪤 CMOD not active = bulb without switch: wired, but no light.
  • 💰 FIBF = "FI Business Functions": copy SAMPLE, register with an ACTIVE product.
  • 🕵️ CL_EXITHANDLER = BADI detector: breakpoint there and run the T-code.

🎮 Your turn (answer first, then tap)

🎤 FULL MODULE ANSWER

"Enhancements add custom logic to SAP standard without modifying it. Older options are user exits in SAP includes and customer exits through SMOD and CMOD; newer ones are BADIs defined in SE18 and implemented as classes in SE19, and the Enhancement Framework with explicit and implicit points and new BADIs in SE20. FI also has BTEs in FIBF. I find sockets via SE93, CALL CUSTOMER-FUNCTION, a CL_EXITHANDLER breakpoint and GET BADI. In MIGO I used MB_MIGO_BADI to block 101 GRs without a storage location and MB_DOCUMENT_BADI to log GRs, always preferring BADIs over implicit enhancements and modifications."

DOHands-on

⚠️ Mostly not possible in BTP: no SMOD/CMOD, no SE18/SE19 screens, no user exits, no implicit enhancements. You can't touch SAP code in ABAP Cloud.

  1. Practice the BADI idea = interfaces: redo Module 6 L9 (interface + class) in ADT.
  2. In S/4HANA Cloud, developers implement released BADIs in ADT (New → BAdI Enhancement Implementation). The free trial has very few to try, so read only.
  1. SE93 → MIGO → program and package. Search the program for CALL CUSTOMER-FUNCTION.
  2. SE24: breakpoint in CL_EXITHANDLER=>GET_INSTANCE → run MIGO → note the BADI names.
  3. SE18 → MB_DOCUMENT_BADI: read the interface and its methods. Don't activate anything in shared systems without approval.
  4. In a sandbox: SE19 implementation with a breakpoint only, post a test GR (101), see it stop. Deactivate after.
  5. Open any Z FM in SE37 → Enhance → Show Implicit Enhancement Options. Just look at the yellow lines.

TESTQuick check

1. Customer exits are implemented in…

SMOD to look, CMOD project to implement and activate.

2. A BADI is implemented as…

SE18 defines the interface, SE19 creates your class.

3. USEREXIT_SAVE_DOCUMENT_PREPARE in MV45AFZZ is a…

USEREXIT_ FORMs = user exits (needs access key).

4. Where are implicit enhancement points?

Implicit = available everywhere without SAP preparation.

5. Breakpoint to find classic BADIs?

Each stop shows a classic BADI name. New BADIs: statement breakpoint GET BADI.

6. BTE T-code?

FIBF: info system, products, P/S and process modules.
Answer all 6 first.
⚡ Quick revise. Read top to bottom in 2 minutes. Forgot something? Switch to 📖 Learn.

🔌 Part 1 · Why (Q19, Q51)

  • Modification = change SAP code (access key, SPAU at upgrade). Enhancement = plug into SAP sockets, upgrade-safe.
  • Order: user exit → customer exit → classic BADI → Enhancement Framework (SE20: implicit, explicit, new BADIs) · BTE for FI.
  • S/4: key user Custom Fields / Custom Logic, released BADIs, side-by-side BTP.

🧓 Part 2 · Exits (Q49)

  • User exit: USEREXIT_… FORM in SAP include (MV45AFZZ), access key, mostly SD.
  • Customer exit: CALL CUSTOMER-FUNCTION → EXIT_prog_nnn → INCLUDE ZX…. SMOD find → CMOD project → activate. Function / screen / menu exits.
  • BADI: interface + class, many implementations, filters.

🧑 Part 3 · BADI

  • SE18 definition (interface IF_EX_…) → SE19 implementation (class ZCL_IM_…) → activate.
  • MIGO: MB_MIGO_BADI (checks, CHECK_ITEM) · MB_DOCUMENT_BADI (at save, XMSEG).
  • Classic: CL_EXITHANDLER. New: GET BADI / CALL BADI, in spot, faster, fallback class.

🔹 Part 4 · Implicit / explicit (Q50)

Implicit: start/end of FORM / FM / method, end of include. Enhance (Shift+F4) → Edit → Enhancement Operations → Show Implicit Enhancement Options → create code implementation → activate. Explicit: POINT = add, SECTION = replace.

💰 Part 5 · BTE & finding (Q52, Q53)

  • BTE: FIBF. P/S = notify, process = replace. Copy SAMPLE_INTERFACE_xxxxxxxx, active product, register.
  • Find: SE93 → program / package → CALL CUSTOMER-FUNCTION → CL_EXITHANDLER=>GET_INSTANCE breakpoint → GET BADI statement breakpoint → SPRO BAdI nodes.

🎭 Part 6 · Scenario (Q69)

Understand → config first → find sockets → new BADI → classic BADI → customer exit → BTE → explicit → implicit → modification last → test → document → transport.

🪤 Traps / PDF fixes: EXIT_SAPMM06E_001 = customer exit, not user exit. FPM is a UI framework, not an enhancement. CMOD inactive → no run. Multiple implementations → no fixed order.
🎤 30-SECOND ANSWER

"Enhancements add logic to SAP standard without modifying it: user exits, customer exits in SMOD and CMOD, BADIs in SE18 and SE19, the Enhancement Framework with implicit and explicit points and new BADIs, and BTEs in FIBF for FI. I find them via SE93, CALL CUSTOMER-FUNCTION and breakpoints on CL_EXITHANDLER and GET BADI. In MIGO I used MB_MIGO_BADI to block 101 GRs without a storage location."

📥
MODULE 08 · PDF Q37–40, Q68 · COMPLETE ✅

BDC (Batch Data Communication)

A program types into SAP screens for you, thousands of times. T-codes: SHDB, SM35, SE38.

😫 PROBLEMBusiness sends an Excel with 2,000 materials to change. Typing them in MM02 by hand = 3 days and many typos.
💡 IDEARecord the screens once. Let a program fill the same screens for every Excel row, with all SAP checks.
🔧 HOWSHDB recording → BDCDATA table → CALL TRANSACTION or session (SM35) → error log.
➡️ NEXTModule 9: Forms (SmartForms, Adobe).

LEARNLevel by level

Part 1 · What and methods (Q37) Part 2 · Call transaction vs session (Q38) Part 3 · Pros and cons (Q39) Part 4 · Errors (Q40, Q68)
PART 1 · WHAT

🤔 What is BDC?

BDC = Batch Data Communication (also called batch input). Your program acts like a user: it opens a transaction, fills the fields, presses Enter and Save. For every row of a file.

  • ❓ Why? Load or change lots of data: go-live migration (old system → SAP) or mass changes.
  • 🕰️ Before: people typed it all by hand. Slow, boring, many mistakes.
  • 🛡️ Why not just UPDATE the table? Never write SAP tables directly. BDC goes through the screens, so every SAP check (does the material exist? valid plant?) still runs, and all related tables are updated correctly.
📦
Our example: Excel with 2,000 materials. For each, change the purchasing group (MARC-EKGRP, the buyer team) in plant 1000 using MM02 (change material).
⚠️ Honest GR note: MIGO uses modern GUI controls, so it can't be recorded well. To post many GRs from a file, use the function BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE + BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT. BDC fits classic screens like MM02.
🧠 Remember: BDC = program types on screens for you. All SAP checks run.

🎥 Step 1: record with SHDB

  1. SHDB → New recording → name ZMM02_EKGRP, transaction MM02.
  2. SAP opens MM02. Do the change once by hand, slowly, with a test material. Use Enter, not the mouse, where you can.
  3. Save. SHDB shows every screen and field you touched.
  4. Button Program → SAP generates an ABAP program from the recording. Copy its screen/field list into your own report.

📋 Step 2: the BDCDATA table

The recording becomes rows of the SAP structure BDCDATA. Each row is either "go to this screen" or "put this value in this field".

FieldMeaningExample
PROGRAMScreen's programSAPLMGMM
DYNPROScreen number0060
DYNBEGIN'X' = a new screen starts hereX
FNAMField nameRMMG1-MATNR
FVALValue to typeMAT-A

🔧 Two little helper FORMs (everyone uses them)

FORM bdc_dynpro USING iv_prog iv_dynp.     " new screen
  APPEND VALUE #( program = iv_prog dynpro = iv_dynp dynbegin = 'X' ) TO gt_bdc.
ENDFORM.
FORM bdc_field USING iv_fnam iv_fval.       " one field
  APPEND VALUE #( fnam = iv_fnam fval = iv_fval ) TO gt_bdc.
ENDFORM.

🔑 gt_bdc = TYPE TABLE OF bdcdata. Special field BDC_OKCODE = which button / key: /00 = Enter, =BU = Save, /11 = F11 (Save). BDC_CURSOR = where the cursor stands.

🧠 Remember: SHDB records → BDCDATA rows (screen row + field rows) → BDC_OKCODE = the key press.

🧰 The 3 BDC methods (Q37)

MethodHowIn simple words
📞 Call TransactionCALL TRANSACTION 'MM02' USING gt_bdcRuns now, row by row. You handle errors yourself
📁 Session methodBDC_OPEN_GROUP → BDC_INSERT → BDC_CLOSE_GROUP, run in SM35Saves everything as a session first. Run it later. SAP logs errors
⚡ Direct inputSAP standard programs (e.g. RMDATIND for materials)Skips screens, SAP's own checks in code. Fast, only for some objects

🆕 What is used today

  • 🧩 BAPI first (e.g. BAPI_MATERIAL_SAVEDATA, BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE): no screens, stable, faster.
  • 🧙 LSMW (Legacy System Migration Workbench): no-code tool using recording / BAPI / IDoc. Old, not recommended in S/4.
  • ☁️ S/4HANA Migration Cockpit (Fiori app "Migrate Your Data", old T-code LTMC): SAP's tool for go-live loads.
🧠 Remember: 3 methods = Call Transaction (now), Session (later, SM35), Direct Input (no screens).
PART 2 · CALL TRANSACTION

📞 Call Transaction method

LOOP AT gt_file INTO DATA(ls_row).              " rows from Excel
  CLEAR gt_bdc.
  PERFORM bdc_dynpro USING 'SAPLMGMM' '0060'.
  PERFORM bdc_field  USING 'RMMG1-MATNR' ls_row-matnr.
  PERFORM bdc_field  USING 'BDC_OKCODE' '/00'.  " Enter
  " ... next screens copied from the SHDB recording
  CALL TRANSACTION 'MM02' USING gt_bdc
       MODE 'N' UPDATE 'S' MESSAGES INTO gt_msg.
ENDLOOP.

🚶 Step by step

  1. gt_file = Excel rows, uploaded with e.g. TEXT_CONVERT_XLS_TO_SAP or GUI_UPLOAD (text file).
  2. For each row: clear gt_bdc, build the screens again with this row's values.
  3. SAPLMGMM 0060 = MM02's first screen; RMMG1-MATNR = its material field.
  4. CALL TRANSACTION runs MM02 with these "keystrokes". gt_msg collects SAP's messages (type BDCMSGCOLL).
MODEMeansUse
AAll screens shownTesting: watch each step
EShow only screens with errorsTest with real data
NNo screensReal run, background
UPDATEMeans
S SynchronousWait until the DB is updated. Safe: next row sees the change. ✅ usual
A AsynchronousDon't wait. Faster, but errors in the update are not seen by your program
L LocalUpdate in the same work process
💡 Modern style: CALL TRANSACTION 'MM02' USING gt_bdc OPTIONS FROM ls_opt with ls_opt TYPE ctu_params (dismode, updmode, defsize = 'X' for table controls).
🧠 Remember: CLEAR gt_bdc per row. MODE A/E/N. UPDATE S is safe. MESSAGES INTO for errors.

📁 Session method

CALL FUNCTION 'BDC_OPEN_GROUP'
  EXPORTING client = sy-mandt  group = 'ZMM02_EKGRP'
            user   = sy-uname  keep  = 'X'.
LOOP AT gt_file INTO DATA(ls_row).
  " build gt_bdc exactly like L4 ...
  CALL FUNCTION 'BDC_INSERT'
    EXPORTING tcode = 'MM02'  TABLES dynprotab = gt_bdc.
ENDLOOP.
CALL FUNCTION 'BDC_CLOSE_GROUP'.

🚶 Step by step

  1. BDC_OPEN_GROUP opens a session (a named folder). keep = 'X' = keep it after it runs, for the log.
  2. BDC_INSERT puts one transaction's screens into the folder. Nothing is posted yet!
  3. BDC_CLOSE_GROUP closes the folder.
  4. SM35 → select session ZMM02_EKGRP → Process (foreground / display errors only / background). Or schedule program RSBDCSUB as a job.
  5. After processing: SM35 → Log shows each transaction, OK or error. Error transactions can be reprocessed after fixing.
🪤 Trap: only one session can be open at a time in a program. And the session always updates synchronously.
🧠 Remember: OPEN → INSERT (per row) → CLOSE → SM35 process + log.

🆚 Call Transaction vs Session (Q38)

📞 Call Transaction📁 Session
When it postsImmediatelyLater, when processed in SM35
UpdateSync or async (your choice)Always synchronous
Error logYou build it (MESSAGES INTO)Automatic log in SM35
Reprocess errorsYou re-run with a fixed fileFix and reprocess in SM35
SpeedFasterSlower
TransactionsOne per CALLMany different T-codes in one session
Return infosy-subrc + messages right awayOnly in the log
Best forSmall / medium loads, need result nowBig loads, night runs, users fix errors
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q37, Q38)

"BDC has three methods: call transaction, session method and direct input. Call transaction posts immediately with CALL TRANSACTION USING the BDCDATA table, lets me choose mode A, E or N and synchronous or asynchronous update, and returns messages that I must log myself. The session method stores transactions with BDC_OPEN_GROUP, BDC_INSERT and BDC_CLOSE_GROUP, is processed later in SM35, always updates synchronously and logs errors automatically. I recorded MM02 in SHDB to change purchasing groups for 2,000 materials and used call transaction with an error file. For bigger loads I used a session so users could reprocess errors in SM35."

🧠 Remember: CT = now, fast, own log. Session = later, slow, SM35 log.
PART 3 · PROS AND CONS

⚖️ Advantages and disadvantages (Q39)

👍 Advantages👎 Disadvantages
All screen checks run → clean dataSlow: goes screen by screen
Works for almost any classic transactionBreaks when the screen changes (upgrade, new field, layout)
Easy start: record in SHDBDoesn't work well with GUI controls / enjoy screens (MIGO, ME21N) and not with Fiori
Related tables updated by SAP itselfTable controls: line count depends on screen size (use defsize = 'X')
Session: automatic log, reprocessingUser settings / defaults can change screens (e.g. MM02 view selection)

🆚 BDC vs BAPI

📥 BDC🧩 BAPI
UsesScreensFunction module, no screens
SpeedSlowFast
Upgrade-safeNoYes (released interface)
ErrorsScreen messagesRETURN table (BAPIRET2)
CommitDone by the transactionYou call BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q39)

"BDC's advantage is that it goes through the screens, so every standard check runs and SAP updates all related tables, and it's quick to build from an SHDB recording. The disadvantages are speed, and that it breaks when screens change after upgrades or user settings, and it can't handle GUI control screens like MIGO or Fiori. So I use a BAPI when one exists, for GR that's BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE with BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT, and BDC only for classic screens like MM02. Once an MM02 BDC failed because a user had different default views, so I made the view selection explicit in the recording."

🧠 Remember: BDC = safe checks but slow and fragile. BAPI first.
PART 4 · ERRORS

🚨 Handling errors (Q40)

📞 In Call Transaction: read the messages

LOOP AT gt_msg INTO DATA(ls_msg) WHERE msgtyp CA 'EA'.
  MESSAGE ID ls_msg-msgid TYPE 'S' NUMBER ls_msg-msgnr
          WITH ls_msg-msgv1 ls_msg-msgv2 ls_msg-msgv3 ls_msg-msgv4
          INTO DATA(lv_text).               " readable text
  APPEND VALUE #( matnr = ls_row-matnr text = lv_text ) TO gt_err.
ENDLOOP.

🚶 Step by step

  1. gt_msg has SAP's messages for this row. msgtyp CA 'EA' = type E (error) or A (abort).
  2. MESSAGE … INTO lv_text turns the message ID + number + variables into a sentence like "Material MAT-X does not exist". (Old way: FM FORMAT_MESSAGE.)
  3. Collect each failed row + text in gt_err.
  4. At the end: show gt_err in ALV (Module 4) and let the user download it, fix it and upload only those rows again.

🔁 Other option: put the failed rows into a session (BDC_INSERT) so users can fix them screen by screen in SM35.

📁 In Session: SM35

  1. SM35 → session → Log: see which transactions failed and why.
  2. Fix master data (or the session data) → select the session → Process again in foreground or "display errors only".
🛡️ Best: stop errors before BDC. Pre-check the file in ABAP: material exists (MARA), plant valid (T001W), purchasing group valid (T024), no empty keys. Bad rows go straight to the error list.
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q40)

"In call transaction I use MESSAGES INTO to collect BDCMSGCOLL messages, pick types E and A, turn them into text with MESSAGE ID INTO or FORMAT_MESSAGE, and write an error list with the input row, shown in ALV and downloadable for correction. Failed rows can also go into a session for manual fixing. In the session method the SM35 log shows errors and the session can be reprocessed. I also validate the file before posting, checking MARA, T001W and T024, so most bad rows never reach the screens."

🧠 Remember: MESSAGES INTO → type E/A → text → error ALV → fix and reload. Pre-check first.

🎭 "BDC fails because of incorrect data. What do you do?" (Q68)

  1. 🛑 Stop and look: don't re-run blindly. Read the error list / SM35 log: which rows, which message?
  2. 🔍 Group the errors: same message on many rows (e.g. "purchasing group 9Z9 does not exist") = one root cause.
  3. 🧑‍💼 Data vs program? Bad data → send the error file back to the business owner to correct. Program bug (wrong field, screen changed) → fix the recording / code and test in mode A.
  4. 🧪 Add pre-checks for that error type so it is caught next time before posting.
  5. 🔁 Re-run only the failed rows (the good ones already posted!). Avoid duplicates: for creates, check if the record already exists.
  6. ✅ Confirm: count posted vs file rows, spot-check in MM03, share the final log.
📦
Example story: 2,000 MM02 rows, 140 failed: 120 had purchasing group 9Z9 (typo for 902), 20 materials weren't extended to plant 1000. Sent the error file to the buyer team, added a T024 and MARC pre-check, re-ran the 140 rows: all posted.
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q68)

"I'd first read the error log to see which rows failed and why, then group errors by message to find root causes. If the data was wrong, I'd return an error file to the business owner; if the program was wrong, for example a changed screen, I'd fix the recording and test in mode A. I'd add pre-validations for those cases and re-run only the failed rows, making sure nothing is posted twice. In one MM02 load 140 of 2,000 rows failed because of a wrong purchasing group and missing plant extension; after the business fixed the file and I added T024 and MARC checks, the re-run posted all of them."

🧠 Remember: read log → group → data or code? → pre-check → re-run failed only → confirm counts.

😂 BDC tricks

  • 🤖 BDC = "Bina Dimaag Clerk": a clerk who types exactly what you recorded, never thinks.
  • 🎥 SHDB = "SHooting DataBase": shoot the video once (record), replay it 2,000 times.
  • 📞 Call Transaction = phone call: answer now. 📁 Session = post office parcel: goes later, with a receipt (SM35 log).
  • 🅰️🅴🅽 MODE A-E-N: "All dikhao, Error dikhao, Nothing dikhao".
  • 📂 Session = OIC: Open, Insert, Close (like a file). Then SM35.
  • 🧩 BAPI before BDC: "B before B, alphabetical": BAPI comes first in the line.

🎮 Your turn (answer first, then tap)

🎤 FULL MODULE ANSWER

"BDC loads data by simulating screen input: I record the transaction in SHDB, build a BDCDATA table per file row and post with call transaction or the session method; direct input is a third, screen-less method for some objects. Call transaction is immediate with my own error log from MESSAGES INTO; sessions run later in SM35 with automatic logs and reprocessing. BDC runs all screen checks but is slow and breaks when screens change, so I prefer BAPIs, like BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE for goods receipts, and use BDC for classic screens like MM02. I always pre-validate the file and re-run only failed rows."

DOHands-on

⚠️ Not possible in BTP: no SAP GUI screens, so no SHDB, no CALL TRANSACTION, no SM35. In the cloud, data is loaded through APIs (OData / RAP) and the Migration Cockpit.

  1. Practice the logic only: class with IF_OO_ADT_CLASSRUN, a table of "file rows", pre-check each row, collect errors in a table, out->write( ) the error list.
  1. Use a test / sandbox client only. SHDB → record MM02 on one test material (change purchasing group in plant view).
  2. SHDB → Program → generate ZMM02_REC. Read the BDCDATA it builds.
  3. Copy into your report with the bdc_dynpro / bdc_field FORMs. Run with MODE 'A' for 2 rows, then 'N'.
  4. Put one bad material in the file → read gt_msg, build the error text, show it in ALV.
  5. Change to the session method → SM35 → process → read the log.

TESTQuick check

1. T-code to record a transaction for BDC?

SHDB = recorder. SM35 = process sessions.

2. Which method always updates synchronously and logs errors automatically?

Session: processed in SM35 with a log. Call transaction: you choose update mode and build the log.

3. MODE 'E' in CALL TRANSACTION means…

A = all, E = errors only, N = none.

4. Where do Call Transaction errors come back?

Read type E / A messages and build your own error list.

5. Best way to post many GRs from a file?

MIGO has GUI controls (bad for BDC). Never update SAP tables directly.

6. 140 of 2,000 rows failed. Next run should…

Good rows are already posted. Avoid duplicates.
Answer all 6 first.
⚡ Quick revise. Read top to bottom in 2 minutes. Forgot something? Switch to 📖 Learn.

📥 Part 1 · What & methods (Q37)

  • BDC = program types on screens for each file row. All SAP checks run. Never UPDATE SAP tables directly.
  • SHDB record → BDCDATA: PROGRAM, DYNPRO, DYNBEGIN (screen) · FNAM, FVAL (field) · BDC_OKCODE /00 Enter, =BU Save.
  • 3 methods: Call Transaction · Session (SM35) · Direct Input (RMDATIND). Today: BAPI, Migration Cockpit (LTMC), LSMW (old).

📞📁 Part 2 · CT vs session (Q38)

CALL TRANSACTION 'MM02' USING gt_bdc MODE 'N' UPDATE 'S' MESSAGES INTO gt_msg.
  • MODE A all / E errors / N none · UPDATE S sync / A async / L local · CTU_PARAMS defsize.
  • Session: BDC_OPEN_GROUP → BDC_INSERT → BDC_CLOSE_GROUP → SM35 (or RSBDCSUB job).
  • CT: now, fast, sync/async, own log. Session: later, slower, always sync, auto log, reprocess, many T-codes.

⚖️ Part 3 · Pros / cons (Q39)

👍 checks run, any classic T-code, easy SHDB, SAP updates all tables. 👎 slow, breaks on screen changes, no GUI controls (MIGO) / Fiori, table control sizes. → BAPI first (GR: BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE + COMMIT).

🚨 Part 4 · Errors (Q40, Q68)

  • CT: MESSAGES INTO → msgtyp E/A → MESSAGE ID … INTO text (or FORMAT_MESSAGE) → error ALV / file. Failed rows → session option.
  • Session: SM35 log → fix → reprocess.
  • Pre-check MARA, T001W, T024 before posting.
  • Q68: read log → group → data or code? → pre-check → re-run failed rows only → confirm counts.
🪤 Traps: forgot CLEAR gt_bdc → wrong screens. Async update → errors not seen. One session open at a time. BDC on MIGO → no, BAPI.
🎤 30-SECOND ANSWER

"BDC simulates screen input: record in SHDB, build BDCDATA per row, then call transaction for immediate posting with my own error log, or the session method processed in SM35 with automatic logs. It runs all screen checks but is slow and fragile, so I prefer BAPIs like BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE for GRs. I pre-validate files and re-run only failed rows."

MODULE 09 · PDF Q32–36 · COMPLETE ✅

Forms: SAPscript, SmartForms, Adobe

Print or PDF a GR slip, PO or invoice in the company layout. T-codes: SE71, SMARTFORMS, SFP, NACE, SO10.

😫 PROBLEMThe warehouse needs a printed GR slip with logo, PO number, items and a signature box. A report list (WRITE / ALV) can't do a nice page layout.
💡 IDEADesign the page once (windows, fonts, logo). A driver program reads the data and fills it.
🔧 HOWSAPscript (SE71) → SmartForms (SMARTFORMS) → Adobe Forms (SFP). Output type in NACE triggers printing.
➡️ NEXTModule 10: CDS views & AMDP.

LEARNLevel by level

Part 1 · The 3 tools (Q32) Part 2 · Migration (Q33) Part 3 · Main window (Q34) Part 4 · Pass values (Q35) Part 5 · Debug (Q36)
PART 1 · FORMS

🤔 What is a form?

A form is a page layout for a business document: GR slip, purchase order, invoice, delivery note. It goes to a printer, PDF or email.

Every form has 2 halves:

⚙️ Driver (print) programreads data, calls →🎨 Form layout→🖨️ printer / PDF / email
  • ⚙️ Driver program = ABAP report: SELECT from MKPF / MSEG / EKKO, then pass the data to the form.
  • 🎨 Form = design only: where the logo goes, which fields, which font, page breaks.

🔔 Who starts the printing? Output types

Usually not a user running a report. When a document is saved, SAP's output control creates a message that calls the driver program + form.

DocumentOutput typeStandard SAPscript / driver
📦 GR slip (MIGO)WE01 / WE02 / WE03WESCHEIN / SAPM07DR
🛒 Purchase order (ME21N)NEUMEDRUCK / SAPFM06P

⚙️ NACE = output config: for each output type, which driver program + form. MB90 = reprint GR slips. Output log in table NAST.

🧠 Remember: form = layout, driver = data. NACE links output type → driver + form.

👴 SAPscript (oldest)

  • 🛠️ T-codes: SE71 form, SE72 styles, SE78 logos / graphics, SO10 standard texts.
  • 📄 Built from pages, windows (MAIN + others), paragraph / character formats and text elements (/E ITEM).
  • 🔢 Client-dependent: must be copied to other clients.
  • ✍️ Text-editor style. No drag & drop. Logic in the driver program.
CALL FUNCTION 'OPEN_FORM'  EXPORTING form = 'ZGR_SLIP'.
LOOP AT gt_mseg INTO gs_mseg.
  CALL FUNCTION 'WRITE_FORM'
    EXPORTING element = 'ITEM'  window = 'MAIN'.
ENDLOOP.
CALL FUNCTION 'CLOSE_FORM'.

🔑 OPEN_FORM = start printing. WRITE_FORM = print the text element ITEM into window MAIN. Inside the element, &GS_MSEG-MATNR& prints the current value. CLOSE_FORM = finish. (START_FORM / END_FORM for many forms in one job.)

🧠 Remember: SAPscript = SE71, client-dependent, OPEN → WRITE → CLOSE, &FIELD&.

🧑 SmartForms (since R/3 4.6C)

  • 🛠️ T-codes: SMARTFORMS form, SMARTSTYLES styles, SE78 graphics.
  • 🖱️ Graphical tree + drag & drop layout. Tables, loops, conditions and even ABAP code (program lines) inside the form.
  • 🌐 Client-independent. Has its own form interface (like FM parameters).
  • ⚙️ When you activate it, SAP generates a function module (name like /1BCDWB/SF00000123).
DATA lv_fm TYPE rs38l_fnam.
CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME'
  EXPORTING formname = 'ZSF_GR_SLIP'
  IMPORTING fm_name  = lv_fm.
CALL FUNCTION lv_fm
  EXPORTING  is_header = gs_mkpf
  TABLES     it_items  = gt_mseg
  EXCEPTIONS OTHERS    = 1.

🚶 Step by step

  1. SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME asks: "what is the generated FM name of form ZSF_GR_SLIP in this system?"
  2. CALL FUNCTION lv_fm (no quotes!) calls that FM with a variable name. RS38L_FNAM = type for FM names.
  3. is_header / it_items = the form's interface parameters. GR header from MKPF, items from MSEG.
🪤 Trap: never hard-code /1BCDWB/SF00000123. The number is different in DEV, QA and PRD. Always ask SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME.
🧠 Remember: SmartForm → generated FM → get its name with SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME → CALL FUNCTION lv_fm.

🧒 Adobe Forms (Interactive Forms by Adobe)

  • 🛠️ T-code: SFP. Two objects: interface (data + code) and form (layout).
  • 🎨 Layout drawn in Adobe LiveCycle Designer (installed on your PC). Output is PDF.
  • 🖥️ Needs ADS (Adobe Document Services, a Java server) to render the PDF. No ADS = no output.
  • ✍️ Interactive PDFs possible: user fills fields in the PDF and SAP reads them back.
  • 🚀 SAP's choice for S/4HANA: new output management uses Adobe forms (e.g. PO form MM_PUR_PURCHASE_ORDER). Cloud: SAP Forms Service by Adobe on BTP.
CALL FUNCTION 'FP_JOB_OPEN'  CHANGING ie_outputparams = ls_out.
CALL FUNCTION 'FP_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME'
  EXPORTING i_name = 'ZAF_GR_SLIP'  IMPORTING e_funcname = lv_fm.
CALL FUNCTION lv_fm
  EXPORTING /1bcdwb/docparams = ls_doc
            is_header         = gs_mkpf
            it_items          = gt_mseg.
CALL FUNCTION 'FP_JOB_CLOSE'.

🔑 Same idea as SmartForms, with a job around it: open job → get FM name → call → close job. ls_out = printer / preview settings. /1bcdwb/docparams = language, country.

🧠 Remember: Adobe = SFP (interface + form), PDF, needs ADS. FP_JOB_OPEN → FP_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME → call → FP_JOB_CLOSE.

🆚 SAPscript vs SmartForms vs Adobe (Q32)

👴 SAPscript🧑 SmartForms🧒 Adobe Forms
T-codeSE71SMARTFORMSSFP
DesignText editorGraphical, drag & dropAdobe LiveCycle Designer
ClientDependentIndependentIndependent
Logic in formVery little (driver does it)Yes: loops, conditions, program linesYes: in the interface + scripting
Main windowsUp to 99 per pageOnly one main windowContent areas (flowing subforms)
Generated FMNoYesYes
OutputPrint / OTFPrint / OTF / PDF / XMLPDF, interactive PDF
NeedsNothing extraNothing extraADS server
TodayLegacyCommon in ECC, maintained⭐ Recommended in S/4
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q32)

"SAPscript, built in SE71, is the oldest: client-dependent, text-editor based, with logic in the driver program using OPEN_FORM, WRITE_FORM and CLOSE_FORM. SmartForms are graphical and client-independent, have a form interface and logic like loops and program lines, and generate a function module that I call after getting its name from SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME. Adobe Forms in SFP use an interface plus a LiveCycle Designer layout, produce PDFs including interactive ones, need Adobe Document Services and are SAP's choice for S/4. I built a GR slip SmartForm triggered by output type WE01. Once the form printed fine in DEV but failed in QA because the generated FM name was hard-coded."

🧠 Remember: SE71 → SMARTFORMS → SFP. Client-dep → independent → PDF + ADS.
PART 2 · MIGRATION

🔄 SAPscript → SmartForm (Q33)

  1. 🎨 Styles first: SMARTSTYLES → Utilities → Convert SAPscript style (from SE72).
  2. 📄 Form: SMARTFORMS → enter new name ZSF_GR_SLIP → Utilities → Migration → Import SAPscript form → give ZGR_SLIP + language.
  3. 🔁 Many forms at once: report FB_MIGRATE_FORM (mass migration).
  4. 🛠️ Fix by hand: pages and windows come over, but text element logic, PERFORM calls inside SAPscript, /: IF conditions and box commands need rework (as loops, conditions, program lines).
  5. 🧾 Form interface: define import / tables parameters (header, items).
  6. ⚙️ Driver program: replace OPEN_FORM / WRITE_FORM / CLOSE_FORM with SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME + CALL FUNCTION lv_fm.
  7. 🔔 NACE: change the output type to the new driver + SmartForm. Test print side by side with the old one.
⚠️ Honest: migration copies the layout, not the logic. Plan time for manual work and a page-by-page comparison.
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q33)

"I convert the style in SMARTSTYLES, then in SMARTFORMS use Utilities, Migration, Import SAPscript form, or FB_MIGRATE_FORM for many forms. The layout, pages and windows come over, but text element logic, PERFORMs and conditions must be rebuilt as loops, conditions and program lines. Then I define the form interface, change the driver program to call the generated FM via SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME and update the output type in NACE. For our GR slip, totals were done by a PERFORM in the old SAPscript and were missing after migration, so I rebuilt them with program lines."

🧠 Remember: style → import form → fix logic → interface → new driver → NACE → compare.
PART 3 · MAIN WINDOW

🪟 Main window vs secondary window (Q34)

A page is cut into windows (boxes). Two kinds:

🪟 Main window🔲 Secondary window
HoldsData that can be long: the item table (all MSEG lines)Fixed info: logo, address, header, footer, page number
Too much content?Flows to the next page automatically (page break)Cut off. No overflow
How manyOnly one per SmartForm (same main window on each page)Many
Tables / loops✅ YesOnly short ones that fit
📦
GR slip with 60 items: page 1 shows logo + header (secondary windows) and items 1–25 (main window). Main window is full → SmartForm moves to page 2 (set as "next page") and prints items 26–50, then page 3 for 51–60. Header windows repeat on each page.

🧩 Other SmartForm nodes

  • 📝 Text · 📊 Table (with header / main / footer lines) · 🔁 Loop · 🔲 Template (fixed grid, no overflow)
  • ⚙️ Program lines (ABAP code) · 🔀 Alternative (IF / ELSE) · 📄 Command (forced page break) · 🖼️ Graphic · 🏠 Address
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q34)

"The main window holds content whose length isn't known, like the item table; when it's full, the SmartForm continues on the next page automatically. A SmartForm has only one main window, while it can have many secondary windows for fixed content like logo, address and header, which are cut off if too long. In our GR slip the MSEG items ran in a table in the main window and the header repeated in secondary windows. Once items vanished because I put the table in a secondary window, so I moved it to the main window."

🧠 Remember: main = one, flows to next page. Secondary = many, fixed, cut off.
PART 4 · PASS VALUES

📨 Passing values to a SmartForm (Q35)

1️⃣ Form interface (from the driver)

TabOur GR slip
ImportIS_HEADER TYPE MKPF (doc no., posting date)
TablesIT_ITEMS LIKE MSEG (all items)
ExportInfo back to the driver (e.g. job output info)

2️⃣ Global definitions (inside the form)

Variables the whole form can use: GS_ITEM TYPE MSEG (current loop row), GV_TOTAL TYPE MENGE_D. Also types and initialization code that runs first.

3️⃣ Show the value in a text node

Write the field between ampersands: &IS_HEADER-MBLNR&, &GS_ITEM-MATNR&, &GS_ITEM-MENGE&. At print time SAP puts in the value.

4️⃣ Compute values with program lines

" program lines node, input: GS_ITEM  output: GV_TOTAL
gv_total = gv_total + gs_item-menge.

⚠️ List every variable you use in the node's input / output parameters, or the code can't see it.

5️⃣ Dynamic texts

  • 📚 Include text: a standard text from SO10 (e.g. warehouse rules) or a document text (PO header text). Change text without changing the form.
  • 🧩 Text module: reusable text in SMARTFORMS, used in many forms.
  • 🔀 Conditions tab on any node: print only if GS_ITEM-BWART = '101'.
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q35)

"The driver program passes data through the form interface: import parameters like the MKPF header and tables like the MSEG items. Inside the form I declare global definitions, loop the items into a work area and print fields as &IS_HEADER-MBLNR& or &GS_ITEM-MATNR& in text nodes. Program lines calculate values like the total quantity, with input and output parameters declared, and include texts from SO10 or text modules make texts changeable without touching the form. Once my total stayed zero because I forgot GV_TOTAL in the program lines' output parameters."

🧠 Remember: interface (in) → global defs → &FIELD& → program lines (declare in/out) → SO10 texts.
PART 5 · DEBUG

🐞 Debugging a SmartForm (Q36)

  1. ⚙️ Driver first: breakpoint before CALL FUNCTION lv_fm. Check the data you pass (is gt_mseg filled?).
  2. 🧩 Program lines: add BREAK-POINT. or BREAK sy-uname. (only stops for you) in a program lines node → activate → print preview → debugger stops inside the form.
  3. 🔎 Generated FM: SMARTFORMS → Environment → Function module name → open it in SE37 → search for your node name → set a breakpoint there.
  4. 📜 SFTRACE: switch on the SmartForms trace, print, then read which nodes ran, conditions true/false, page breaks.
  5. 🧹 Remove all BREAK-POINT statements before transport (ATC will warn you).

👴 SAPscript & 🧒 Adobe

  • SAPscript: SE71 → Utilities → Activate debugger (or report RSTXDBUG). Steps through each text element.
  • Adobe: breakpoint in the interface code (SFP → Interface → initialization / code), and check the ADS connection if no PDF comes.
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q36)

"I first debug the driver program to check the data passed to the generated FM. Inside the form I add BREAK sy-uname in a program lines node, or find the generated function module via Environment, Function module name, and set a breakpoint in SE37 near my node. SFTRACE shows which nodes and conditions ran. For SAPscript I use the SE71 form debugger, for Adobe the interface code. Once a column was always empty; SFTRACE showed its condition was false because I compared BWART with 101 without quotes, so I fixed the condition."

🧠 Remember: driver → BREAK in program lines → generated FM in SE37 → SFTRACE. Remove breaks.

😂 Forms tricks

  • 👴🧑🧒 Family: Dada SAPscript (typewriter ⌨️), Papa SmartForms (drag & drop 🖱️), Beta Adobe (PDF 📄).
  • 🔢 SE71 → SMARTFORMS → SFP: "Seventy-one ka typewriter, Smart papa, Fresh PDF beta".
  • 🧾 Driver = cook, form = plate: the cook fills the food (data), the plate decides how it looks.
  • 🪟 Main window = Ganga: flows on and on to the next page. Secondary = matka (pot): fixed size, extra spills out.
  • 🔤 &FIELD& = "ampersand sandwich": field in the middle, & bread on both sides 🥪.
  • 🏷️ Never hard-code /1BCDWB/: "naam har system mein badalta hai" (name changes in every system).

🎮 Your turn (answer first, then tap)

🎤 FULL MODULE ANSWER

"Forms print business documents: a driver program reads the data and passes it to a layout, triggered by output types configured in NACE. SAPscript in SE71 is client-dependent and uses OPEN_FORM, WRITE_FORM and CLOSE_FORM; SmartForms are graphical, client-independent and generate an FM called via SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME; Adobe Forms in SFP produce PDFs through ADS and are the S/4 standard. A SmartForm has one main window that flows across pages and many fixed secondary windows. I pass data through the form interface, compute in program lines and debug with BREAK sy-uname, the generated FM and SFTRACE. I built a GR slip SmartForm for output type WE01 and migrated an old SAPscript to it."

DOHands-on

⚠️ Not possible in the BTP trial: no SE71, no SMARTFORMS, no SFP screens. In the cloud, forms are Adobe templates rendered by the SAP Forms Service by Adobe and triggered by S/4 output management.

  1. NACE → application ME → output type NEU: see driver SAPFM06P + form MEDRUCK. Look up the GR slip types (WE01).
  2. SMARTFORMS: create ZSF_GR_SLIP. Interface: import IS_HEADER TYPE MKPF, tables IT_ITEMS LIKE MSEG.
  3. Main window: table node looping IT_ITEMS into GS_ITEM, cells with &GS_ITEM-MATNR&, &GS_ITEM-MENGE&. Secondary window: &IS_HEADER-MBLNR&.
  4. SE38 driver: read one 101 GR (MKPF + MSEG), call via SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME, print preview.
  5. Add a program lines node with BREAK sy-uname. and a total. Debug, then try SFTRACE. Remove the break.

TESTQuick check

1. Which form type is client-dependent?

SAPscript (SE71) must be copied per client.

2. How do you get a SmartForm's FM name?

SSF_ for SmartForms, FP_ for Adobe. Never hard-code.

3. What does Adobe Forms need to create the PDF?

ADS is a Java server that renders the PDF.

4. How many main windows can a SmartForm have?

One main window that flows across pages. SAPscript allowed up to 99.

5. A program lines node can't see GV_TOTAL. Why?

Declare every used variable in the node's input / output.

6. Tool to mass-migrate SAPscripts?

SFTRACE = SmartForms trace. RSTXDBUG = SAPscript debugger.
Answer all 6 first.

🧾 Part 1 · 3 tools (Q32)

  • SAPscript: SE71 / SE72 / SE78 / SO10, client-dependent, OPEN_FORM → WRITE_FORM (element, window) → CLOSE_FORM.
