MODULE 10 · PDF Q55, Q56, Q58 (Q57 in HANA tab) · COMPLETE ✅

CDS, AMDP, Table Functions

Build data models inside the database and push heavy logic down to HANA. Tool: Eclipse ADT (not SE11 / SE38).

😫 PROBLEMThe same GR join (MKPF/MSEG + EKKO) is written again in 10 reports, Fiori apps and OData services.
💡 IDEADefine the data model once, saved in the database layer (CDS). For very hard logic, write SQLScript that runs inside HANA (AMDP).
🔧 HOWdefine view entity + annotations + associations · AMDP class (IF_AMDP_MARKER_HDB) · CDS table function.
➡️ NEXTModule 11: OData & Workflow (CDS feeds OData / RAP).
🔗 Start with the HANA tab (L6 SQL family, L7 code pushdown). This module goes one step deeper.

LEARNLevel by level

Part 1 · CDS views (Q56) Part 2 · AMDP (Q55) Part 3 · Table functions (Q58)
PART 1 · CDS

🤔 What is CDS?

CDS = Core Data Services. A CDS view is a SELECT that is saved as an object in the system (like a DDIC view, but far more powerful). Any program, Fiori app or OData service can read it by name.

  • ❓ Why? Write the GR join, field names, filters and calculations once. Everyone reuses the same model.
  • 🕰️ Before: Open SQL joins copied into every report, or SE11 database views (inner join only, no calculations).
  • 🚀 Code pushdown: CDS runs in the database, so HANA does the work (HANA tab L7).
  • 🧩 Foundation of S/4HANA: Fiori apps, analytics, RAP and OData are all built on CDS. SAP ships thousands (names start with I_, e.g. I_MaterialDocumentItem).

🛠️ Where do I create it?

Only in Eclipse ADT: right-click package → New → Other ABAP Repository Object → Data Definition. Not in SE11 / SE38. Press F8 for Data Preview.

🧠 Remember: CDS = saved SELECT in the DB layer. Write once, reuse everywhere. Made in ADT.

🔧 Your first CDS view entity

@AccessControl.authorizationCheck: #NOT_REQUIRED
@EndUserText.label: 'GR items, movement type 101'
define view entity ZI_GR_Item
  as select from matdoc
{
  key mblnr as MaterialDocument,
  key mjahr as DocumentYear,
  key zeile as DocumentItem,
      matnr as Material,
      ebeln as PurchaseOrder,
      @Semantics.quantity.unitOfMeasure: 'Unit'
      menge as Quantity,
      meins as Unit
}
where bwart = '101'

🚶 What each line means

  • @… = annotations: extra info for the system. authorizationCheck = should a role (DCL) filter rows? EndUserText.label = description.
  • define view entity ZI_GR_Item = the name. ZI_ = custom Interface view (basic layer).
  • as select from matdoc = source table (S/4). On ECC use mseg.
  • key = key fields of the view. as Material = nice CamelCase names instead of German short names.
  • @Semantics.quantity.unitOfMeasure = Quantity belongs to field Unit (needed for amounts / quantities).
  • where bwart = '101' = only GRs for POs.

📞 Use it in ABAP

SELECT FROM zi_gr_item
  FIELDS material, purchaseorder, quantity
  WHERE purchaseorder = @p_ebeln
  INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_gr).
🕰️ Old style you'll see: @AbapCatalog.sqlViewName: 'ZVGRITEM' + define view. That also created an SE11 SQL view. The new define view entity doesn't need it: simpler, faster to activate, recommended.
🧠 Remember: annotations @ · define view entity · as select from · key · aliases · where. F8 = preview.

🔗 Associations: joins on demand

An association is a prepared link to another view or table. The join happens only if someone uses it.

define view entity ZI_GR_Item
  as select from matdoc
  association [0..1] to ekko as _PO
    on _PO.ebeln = $projection.PurchaseOrder
{ key mblnr as MaterialDocument,
  ...
      ebeln as PurchaseOrder,
      _PO                         // expose the link
}
  • [0..1] = cardinality: each GR line has 0 or 1 PO header.
  • _PO = association name (starts with _ by convention).
  • $projection.PurchaseOrder = "the field PurchaseOrder of this view".

📞 Follow it with a path: in CDS _PO.lifnr, in ABAP SQL \_po-lifnr:

SELECT FROM zi_gr_item
  FIELDS material, \_po-lifnr AS vendor
  INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_vendor).

🎛️ Parameters

define view entity ZI_GR_ByDate
  with parameters p_from : budat, p_to : budat
  as select from matdoc { ... }
  where budat between $parameters.p_from and $parameters.p_to

ABAP: SELECT FROM zi_gr_bydate( p_from = @lv_from, p_to = @lv_to ) …

🏗️ Layers (VDM: Virtual Data Model)

LayerPrefixJob
Interface (basic / composite)I_ / ZI_Clean reusable data, associations
ConsumptionC_ / ZC_For one app: UI annotations, OData exposure

🔐 Access control (DCL): define role filters rows by authorization (e.g. only my plants). Turned on with #CHECK.

🧠 Remember: association = lazy join (_Name, [0..1], $projection). Path: CDS _PO.x, ABAP \_po-x.

🆚 CDS view vs normal SQL query (Q56)

🧾 Open SQL in a report🧠 CDS view
LivesInside one programOwn object in the DB layer, reusable
ReuseCopy the codeAny program / Fiori / OData reads it by name
Extra meaningNoneAnnotations: labels, units, UI, analytics, OData
RelationsJOIN written every timeAssociations, used only when needed
SecurityAUTHORITY-CHECK in ABAPDCL roles filter rows automatically
Built inSE38 / ADTADT only
ExtensibleNoYes: extend view entity adds fields

🆚 CDS view vs SE11 database view

SE11 view: inner join only, no calculations, no parameters. CDS: all joins, CASE, CAST, aggregates (SUM, COUNT), unions, parameters, associations, annotations.

🎤 INTERVIEW (Q56)

"CDS, Core Data Services, lets me define a data model as a saved view in the database layer using define view entity in ADT. Unlike an Open SQL query inside one program, a CDS view is reusable by any program, Fiori app or OData service, carries annotations for labels, units and UI, uses associations instead of repeating joins, and supports access control through DCL roles. I built ZI_GR_Item on MATDOC for movement type 101 with an association to EKKO, and several reports and a Fiori app reused it. Once quantities showed without units in Fiori because I forgot the unitOfMeasure annotation."

🧠 Remember: SQL = one-time question. CDS = saved, reusable model with meaning.
PART 2 · AMDP

🤔 What is AMDP? (Q55)

AMDP = ABAP Managed Database Procedure. A method of an ABAP class whose code is written in SQLScript (HANA's own language) and runs inside HANA. ABAP manages it: it's transported, versioned and called like any method.

  • ❓ Why? Some logic is too complex for Open SQL or CDS: many steps, temporary tables, ranking, complex calculations on millions of rows.
  • 🕰️ Before: pull all rows to ABAP and loop, or write HANA procedures by hand in the DB (not transported with ABAP).
  • ⚠️ Use last: Open SQL → CDS → AMDP.
CLASS zcl_gr_amdp DEFINITION PUBLIC.
  PUBLIC SECTION.
    INTERFACES if_amdp_marker_hdb.
    TYPES: BEGIN OF ty_top, matnr TYPE matnr, qty TYPE menge_d, END OF ty_top,
           tt_top TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_top WITH EMPTY KEY.
    CLASS-METHODS top_materials
      IMPORTING VALUE(iv_mandt) TYPE mandt
      EXPORTING VALUE(et_top)  TYPE tt_top.
ENDCLASS.
CLASS zcl_gr_amdp IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD top_materials BY DATABASE PROCEDURE FOR HDB
                       LANGUAGE SQLSCRIPT OPTIONS READ-ONLY USING mseg.
    et_top = SELECT matnr, SUM( menge ) AS qty FROM mseg
             WHERE mandt = :iv_mandt AND bwart = '101'
             GROUP BY matnr ORDER BY qty DESC LIMIT 10;
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

🚶 What each line means

  • INTERFACES if_amdp_marker_hdb = "this class has HANA methods". Required.
  • BY DATABASE PROCEDURE FOR HDB LANGUAGE SQLSCRIPT = the body is SQLScript, run in HANA (HDB).
  • OPTIONS READ-ONLY = only reads. USING mseg = every table used must be listed.
  • :iv_mandt = colon before a parameter inside SQLScript. Each statement ends with ;.
  • Result: top 10 materials by GR quantity, calculated fully inside HANA.

📞 Call: zcl_gr_amdp=>top_materials( EXPORTING iv_mandt = sy-mandt IMPORTING et_top = DATA(lt_top) ).

🧠 Remember: AMDP = class method in SQLScript, IF_AMDP_MARKER_HDB, BY DATABASE PROCEDURE, USING tables.

📏 AMDP rules and traps

RuleWhy
All parameters VALUE( )Passed by value into the DB
Only IMPORTING / EXPORTING / CHANGING, no RETURNINGProcedure rule
Parameters: elementary types or tables of elementary fieldsNo nested structures / objects
Client not automatic: filter mandt yourselfOpen SQL adds the client for you; SQLScript doesn't
Only for HANA (FOR HDB)Won't run on Oracle / DB2
List all tables in USINGSo ABAP knows dependencies
Only in ADT (Eclipse)SE24 can't edit the SQLScript body

🐞 Debugging

ADT → set a breakpoint in the SQLScript line (AMDP debugger, needs debug authorization) → run the ABAP that calls it. You can see intermediate table variables.

🎤 INTERVIEW (Q55)

"AMDP is an ABAP class method implemented in SQLScript that runs inside HANA, while ABAP still manages, transports and calls it. The class implements IF_AMDP_MARKER_HDB and the method uses BY DATABASE PROCEDURE FOR HDB LANGUAGE SQLSCRIPT with a USING list of tables; parameters are by value and there's no RETURNING. I use it only when Open SQL and CDS aren't enough, for example ranking the top GR materials across millions of MSEG rows. Once my AMDP returned data from all clients because SQLScript doesn't add the client automatically, so I passed sy-mandt and filtered on MANDT."

🧠 Remember: VALUE( ) always, no RETURNING, filter MANDT yourself, HANA only, ADT only.
PART 3 · TABLE FUNCTIONS

🤔 What is a CDS table function? (Q58)

A CDS table function looks like a CDS view from outside (you SELECT from it), but inside, its data comes from an AMDP function written in SQLScript. It's the bridge: CDS outside, AMDP inside.

  • ❓ Why? When a normal CDS view can't express the logic, but apps / OData still need a CDS-style source.

1️⃣ Define it (DDL in ADT)

@EndUserText.label: 'Top GR materials'
define table function ZTF_GR_Top
  with parameters
    @Environment.systemField: #CLIENT
    p_client : abap.clnt
  returns { client : abap.clnt;
            matnr  : matnr;
            qty    : abap.dec(15,3); }
  implemented by method zcl_gr_tf=>get_top;

2️⃣ Implement it (AMDP function)

CLASS zcl_gr_tf DEFINITION PUBLIC.
  PUBLIC SECTION.
    INTERFACES if_amdp_marker_hdb.
    CLASS-METHODS get_top FOR TABLE FUNCTION ztf_gr_top.
ENDCLASS.
CLASS zcl_gr_tf IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD get_top BY DATABASE FUNCTION FOR HDB
                 LANGUAGE SQLSCRIPT OPTIONS READ-ONLY USING mseg.
    RETURN SELECT mandt AS client, matnr, SUM( menge ) AS qty
           FROM mseg WHERE mandt = :p_client AND bwart = '101'
           GROUP BY mandt, matnr;
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

🚶 What each part means

  • returns { … } = the columns it gives back. First field = client (client-dependent function).
  • @Environment.systemField: #CLIENT = SAP fills p_client with the logon client automatically.
  • FOR TABLE FUNCTION + BY DATABASE FUNCTION (not PROCEDURE) + RETURN SELECT ….
  • Use: SELECT * FROM ztf_gr_top INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_top). Or use it as a source inside another CDS view.
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q58)

"A CDS table function is defined with define table function, declaring parameters and a returns structure, and is implemented by an AMDP function: a class implementing IF_AMDP_MARKER_HDB with a method FOR TABLE FUNCTION, coded BY DATABASE FUNCTION in SQLScript with RETURN SELECT. From outside it's consumed like a CDS view, in ABAP SQL or other CDS views. I used one for top GR materials where the logic was too complex for a normal view. Data was missing until I mapped the client field first and used the CLIENT system field annotation."

🧠 Remember: table function = CDS shell + AMDP function inside. BY DATABASE FUNCTION + RETURN SELECT.

🧭 Which tool when?

NeedTool
One report, simple join / SUM🧒 New Open SQL (ABAP SQL)
Reusable model, Fiori / OData / analytics🧠 CDS view entity
Reusable model but logic too complex for CDS🧩 CDS table function (+ AMDP)
Complex multi-step calculation called from ABAP⚡ AMDP procedure
Open SQL→CDS view→Table function→AMDP

Go right only when the left tool can't do it. Each step right = more power, less portability, harder to read.

🆚 AMDP procedure vs table function

⚡ AMDP procedure🧩 Table function
Called fromABAP method callSELECT / other CDS views
KeywordBY DATABASE PROCEDUREBY DATABASE FUNCTION + FOR TABLE FUNCTION
OutputEXPORTING tables (several)One result set (RETURN)
Can change dataYes (without READ-ONLY)No, read only
🧠 Remember: SQL → CDS → TF → AMDP. Procedure = called by ABAP. Function = selected like a view.

😂 CDS / AMDP tricks

  • 📜 CDS = saved recipe: write once, every kitchen (report, Fiori, OData) cooks it.
  • 🔗 Association = "number saved, call when needed": the join happens only when you dial it.
  • 💲 $projection / $parameters = "dollar = this view's own stuff".
  • 🇮 🇨 I_ = Inside (interface), C_ = Customer-facing (consumption).
  • 🏃 AMDP = ABAP Manages, Database Performs.
  • 🏷️ No auto-MANDT in AMDP: "client ka dhyan khud rakho" (take care of the client yourself).
  • 🍔 Table function = burger: CDS bun outside, AMDP patty inside.

🎮 Your turn (answer first, then tap)

🎤 FULL MODULE ANSWER

"On HANA I push logic down step by step. New Open SQL handles simple aggregates; CDS view entities in ADT give reusable models with annotations, associations, parameters and DCL security, and they feed Fiori and OData. When CDS can't express the logic, a CDS table function implemented by an AMDP function returns a view-like result, and AMDP procedures in SQLScript handle complex calculations called from ABAP, with by-value parameters, a USING list and manual client handling. I built ZI_GR_Item on MATDOC with an association to EKKO and an AMDP for top GR materials."

DOHands-on

✅ BTP is the best place for this. MATDOC isn't there, so use your own table.

  1. ADT: table ZGR_ITEM (client, mblnr, zeile, matnr, ebeln, menge, meins, bwart). Fill test rows from a console class.
  2. New → Data Definition → ZI_GR_ITEM on zgr_item with where bwart = '101'. Activate → F8 preview.
  3. Add a parameter or an association to a second Z table (e.g. ZPO_HEADER). Preview again.
  4. Try the AMDP class on zgr_item and call it from your console class. If the trial blocks a step, read the code only.
  1. Check: is your office system on HANA (System → Status → Database)? AMDP needs HANA; CDS needs NetWeaver 7.40+.
  2. Connect Eclipse ADT to the office system (ask Basis for the SAP GUI logon entry).
  3. Create ZI_GR_ITEM on mseg (ECC) or matdoc (S/4). F8 preview.
  4. Use it in your GR ALV report instead of the MKPF + MSEG join. Compare with ST05.

TESTQuick check

1. Where do you create CDS views?

CDS = Data Definition in ADT only.

2. What does an association do?

Lazy join: path _PO.x / \_po-x triggers it.

3. Required interface for an AMDP class?

Marker interface for HANA DB procedures.

4. An AMDP method can NOT have…

No RETURNING, and all parameters must be VALUE( ).

5. A CDS table function is implemented by…

CDS shell outside, AMDP function inside with RETURN SELECT.

6. Best first choice for a simple SUM in one report?

Open SQL → CDS → table function → AMDP. Go right only when needed.
Answer all 6 first.
⚡ Quick revise. Read top to bottom in 2 minutes. Forgot something? Switch to 📖 Learn.

🧠 Part 1 · CDS (Q56)

define view entity ZI_GR_Item as select from matdoc
  association [0..1] to ekko as _PO on _PO.ebeln = $projection.PurchaseOrder
{ key mblnr as MaterialDocument, ebeln as PurchaseOrder, _PO }
where bwart = '101'
  • Saved SELECT in DB layer, made in ADT, F8 preview. Annotations @, aliases, keys, associations (lazy join), parameters ($parameters), DCL roles, extend view.
  • vs Open SQL: reusable, semantics, associations, security, extensible. vs SE11 view: all joins, CASE, SUM, unions, params.
  • Old: @AbapCatalog.sqlViewName + define view. New: define view entity. VDM: I_ interface, C_ consumption.

⚡ Part 2 · AMDP (Q55)

  • Class method in SQLScript, runs in HANA, managed by ABAP. INTERFACES if_amdp_marker_hdb.
  • METHOD m BY DATABASE PROCEDURE FOR HDB LANGUAGE SQLSCRIPT OPTIONS READ-ONLY USING mseg. · :param · ;
  • Rules: VALUE( ) params, no RETURNING, filter MANDT yourself, HANA only, ADT only, list tables in USING. Debug in ADT.

🍔 Part 3 · Table function (Q58)

  • DDL: define table function … with parameters @Environment.systemField: #CLIENT p_client … returns { client … } implemented by method zcl=>m;
  • Class: FOR TABLE FUNCTION, BY DATABASE FUNCTION, RETURN SELECT …; · consumed like a CDS view.
  • Ladder: Open SQL → CDS → table function → AMDP. Procedure = ABAP call, several outputs. Function = SELECT, one result, read only.
🪤 Traps: CDS in SE11? No. AMDP adds client automatically? No. RETURNING in AMDP? No. Quantity without unit annotation → wrong display.
🎤 30-SECOND ANSWER

"CDS view entities, built in ADT, are reusable database-layer models with annotations, associations, parameters and access control, feeding reports, Fiori and OData. When CDS isn't enough, a table function implemented by an AMDP function returns a view-like result, and AMDP procedures run SQLScript inside HANA for complex logic, with by-value parameters and manual client handling. I built ZI_GR_Item on MATDOC and an AMDP for top GR materials."

🌐
MODULE 11 · PDF Q59–62 · COMPLETE ✅

OData & Workflow

OData: give SAP data to Fiori, mobile and other systems over the web. Workflow: send work to the right person automatically. T-codes: SEGW, /IWFND/MAINT_SERVICE, SWDD, SBWP.

😫 PROBLEMThe warehouse wants GRs on a phone (Fiori). And big GRs must be approved by a manager, but people forget to tell them.
💡 IDEAOData: a web URL that returns SAP data as JSON. Workflow: SAP itself sends a task to the manager's inbox.
🔧 HOWSEGW project + DPC_EXT methods, register, test in Gateway client · SWDD workflow + event linkage + SBWP inbox.
➡️ NEXTModule 12: Jobs, LUW, parallel processing, transport.

LEARNLevel by level

Part 1 · OData service (Q60) Part 2 · CRUD (Q61) Part 3 · Debug OData (Q62) Part 4 · Workflow (Q59)
PART 1 · ODATA

🤔 What is OData? (Q60)

OData (Open Data Protocol) is a web standard for reading and writing data with normal HTTP calls (like opening a website). The answer comes back as JSON or XML.

  • ❓ Why? Fiori apps, mobile apps and other systems can't call ABAP function modules directly. They speak HTTP. OData is SAP's bridge.
  • 🕰️ Before: RFC / BAPI calls (SAP-only protocol), IDocs, or SOAP web services (heavy XML).
  • 🧱 In SAP the OData layer is called SAP Gateway (/IWFND/ = frontend hub, /IWBEP/ = backend).

🔗 Read an OData URL

/sap/opu/odata/sap/ZGR_SRV/GrItemSet?$filter=Ebeln eq '4500001234'&$top=10&$format=json
PartMeaning
/sap/opu/odata/sap/Standard path for Gateway services
ZGR_SRVService name
GrItemSetEntity set = a list (like a table) of entities (rows) of type GrItem
$filter, $top, $formatQuery options: only this PO, 10 rows, as JSON
$metadataDescribes all entity types, fields and keys
🧠 Remember: OData = SAP data over HTTP as JSON. Service → entity set → entities. $metadata = the menu.

🛠️ Create an OData service in SEGW (classic way)

  1. SEGW (Gateway Service Builder) → Create project ZGR.
  2. Data Model → Import → DDIC Structure (e.g. a Z structure with MBLNR, ZEILE, MATNR, MENGE, EBELN) → creates entity type GrItem + entity set GrItemSet. Mark the keys.
  3. (Optional) Associations / navigation: PO header → GR items.
  4. Generate Runtime Objects (red-white wheel). SAP creates 4 classes:
    • ZCL_ZGR_MPC + ZCL_ZGR_MPC_EXT = Model Provider (the structure, $metadata)
    • ZCL_ZGR_DPC + ZCL_ZGR_DPC_EXT = Data Provider (the code that reads / writes data)
  5. Write your code in DPC_EXT: redefine methods like GRITEMSET_GET_ENTITYSET (L3).
  6. Register: /IWFND/MAINT_SERVICE → Add Service → system alias (often LOCAL) → find ZGR_SRV → Add.
  7. Test: /IWFND/GW_CLIENT → URL /sap/opu/odata/sap/ZGR_SRV/GrItemSet → Execute → HTTP 200 + data.
🪤 Trap: only change the _EXT classes. The others are regenerated every time you click Generate, and your code there is lost.
🧠 Remember: SEGW → model → generate MPC/DPC → code in DPC_EXT → register /IWFND/MAINT_SERVICE → test /IWFND/GW_CLIENT.

🔧 DPC_EXT: read GR items

METHOD gritemset_get_entityset.         " redefined in ZCL_ZGR_DPC_EXT
  DATA(lv_where) = io_tech_request_context->get_osql_where_clause_convert( ).
  SELECT mblnr, zeile, matnr, menge, ebeln FROM mseg
    WHERE bwart = '101' AND (lv_where)
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE @et_entityset
    UP TO 100 ROWS.
ENDMETHOD.

🚶 What each line means

  • gritemset_get_entityset = "someone called GET on GrItemSet". SAP generated the empty method; you redefine it (Module 6 L7).
  • get_osql_where_clause_convert( ) turns the URL's $filter=Ebeln eq '…' into an ABAP WHERE text.
  • (lv_where) = dynamic WHERE in brackets. Empty = no extra filter.
  • et_entityset = the rows you send back. SAP turns them into JSON.

☝️ Single entity: GET_ENTITY

METHOD gritemset_get_entity.
  READ TABLE it_key_tab INTO DATA(ls_key) WITH KEY name = 'Mblnr'.
  SELECT SINGLE mblnr, zeile, matnr, menge FROM mseg
    WHERE mblnr = @ls_key-value  " + Mjahr, Zeile the same way
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF @er_entity.
ENDMETHOD.

🔑 URL: GrItemSet(Mblnr='5000001234',Mjahr='2026',Zeile='0001'). Keys arrive in it_key_tab (name + value). One row goes back in er_entity.

🪤 Trap: always limit rows (UP TO … ROWS or handle $top / $skip). A Fiori list that asks for all MSEG rows can kill the system.
🧠 Remember: GET_ENTITYSET → et_entityset. GET_ENTITY → it_key_tab → er_entity.

🚀 Modern way: CDS + RAP (no SEGW)

In S/4HANA and BTP you don't write DPC code for simple services. You expose a CDS view (Module 10).

  1. CDS view ZC_GR_Item (consumption view on ZI_GR_Item), with UI annotations.
  2. Service definition (ADT): define service ZUI_GR { expose ZC_GR_Item as GrItem; }
  3. Service binding (ADT): choose OData V4 – UI (or V2) → Activate → Publish.
  4. Click Preview: a Fiori elements list report opens. No UI code!
  5. Create / update / delete: add a behavior definition (RAP: RESTful ABAP Programming model).
🕰️ SEGW🚀 RAP
ModelDDIC structure in SEGWCDS view
CodeDPC_EXT methods for each operationMostly none for read; behavior for CUD
Publish/IWFND/MAINT_SERVICEService binding → Publish
ODataV2V2 or V4
Cloud (BTP)NoYes ✅

🕰️ In between (7.50): @OData.publish: true on a CDS view generated a service. Now deprecated, use RAP.

🎤 INTERVIEW (Q60)

"OData is a REST-based protocol to read and write data over HTTP in JSON or XML, used by Fiori and external apps through SAP Gateway. Classically I create a SEGW project, import a DDIC structure as entity type and set, generate the MPC and DPC classes, code the operations in DPC_EXT, register the service in /IWFND/MAINT_SERVICE and test in /IWFND/GW_CLIENT. In S/4 and BTP I expose a CDS view with a service definition and binding in RAP. I built ZGR_SRV for GR items for a warehouse Fiori app. My code once disappeared because I wrote it in the DPC class instead of DPC_EXT, which gets regenerated."

🧠 Remember: RAP = CDS → service definition → service binding → publish → preview.
PART 2 · CRUD

🔁 CRUD operations (Q61)

CRUD = Create, Read, Update, Delete. Each one is an HTTP method, and each lands in one DPC_EXT method.

CRUDHTTPURL exampleDPC_EXT method
📖 Read listGETGrItemSet…_GET_ENTITYSET
☝️ Read oneGETGrItemSet(Mblnr='…',…)…_GET_ENTITY
➕ CreatePOSTGrItemSet + JSON body…_CREATE_ENTITY
✏️ UpdatePUT / PATCH (MERGE)GrItemSet(…) + body…_UPDATE_ENTITY
🗑️ DeleteDELETEGrItemSet(…)…_DELETE_ENTITY

➕ More: deep insert (header + items in one POST → CREATE_DEEP_ENTITY), $batch (many calls in one request, change sets = one LUW), function import (custom action like "Post GR" → EXECUTE_ACTION), media (files → GET_STREAM).

🎛️ Query options

OptionExampleDoes
$filterEbeln eq '4500001234'WHERE
$selectMatnr,MengeOnly these fields
$top / $skip$top=20&$skip=40Paging
$orderbyMenge descSort
$expandToItemsAlso return linked entities (PO → GR items)
$count / $inlinecount$inlinecount=allpagesTotal number of rows
🪤 CSRF trap: POST / PUT / DELETE need an X-CSRF-Token. First send a GET with header X-CSRF-Token: Fetch, then send that token with the change request. Missing → 403 Forbidden.
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q61)

"CRUD maps to HTTP methods: GET reads an entity set or a single entity by key, POST creates, PUT or PATCH updates and DELETE deletes; in SEGW they land in GET_ENTITYSET, GET_ENTITY, CREATE_ENTITY, UPDATE_ENTITY and DELETE_ENTITY in the DPC_EXT class. There's also deep insert, $batch and function imports. Query options like $filter, $select, $top, $skip, $orderby and $expand shape the result. For our GR service the first POST failed with 403 until I fetched an X-CSRF token with a GET and sent it back."

🧠 Remember: GET read · POST create · PUT/PATCH update · DELETE delete. Changes need a CSRF token.
PART 3 · DEBUG

🐞 Debugging an OData service (Q62)

  1. 🔴 External breakpoint (not a session breakpoint!) in your DPC_EXT method. In SE80/ADT: Utilities → Settings → Debugging → user = the user who calls the service. HTTP calls don't come from your SAP GUI session.
  2. ▶️ Call the service from /IWFND/GW_CLIENT (or the Fiori app). The debugger opens in a new window.
  3. 📜 No stop? Read the logs:
    • /IWFND/ERROR_LOG = Gateway hub errors (with the request and "Replay").
    • /IWBEP/ERROR_LOG = backend errors (your DPC code).
    • /IWFND/TRACES = record full request / response payloads for a user.
  4. 🧹 Changed the model but old fields still show? Clear metadata cache: /IWFND/CACHE_CLEANUP and /IWBEP/CACHE_CLEANUP.
  5. 🌐 Fiori side: browser F12 → Network: see the URL, HTTP status (200, 400, 403, 500) and the error JSON.
  6. 🐌 Slow? SAT / ST05 on the service user (Module 5).
HTTP statusUsually means
200 / 201 / 204OK / created / done, no content
400Bad request: wrong field name or filter syntax
403No authorization or CSRF token missing
404Service / entity not found (not registered? wrong key?)
500Dump or exception in your DPC code → ST22 + error log
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q62)

"I set an external breakpoint for the calling user in the DPC_EXT method, because OData requests come over HTTP and ignore session breakpoints, then trigger it from /IWFND/GW_CLIENT. If it doesn't stop, I check /IWFND/ERROR_LOG on the hub and /IWBEP/ERROR_LOG in the backend, use /IWFND/TRACES for payloads, clear the metadata cache after model changes, and read the HTTP status in the browser's network tab. Once a new field didn't appear in Fiori until I ran the cache cleanup."

🧠 Remember: external breakpoint · GW_CLIENT · ERROR_LOG (IWFND + IWBEP) · CACHE_CLEANUP · F12.
PART 4 · WORKFLOW

🤔 What is SAP Business Workflow? (Q59)

A workflow automates a business process with people steps: "when X happens, send a task to the right person, wait for their decision, then do Y".

  • ❓ Why? Approvals and notifications shouldn't depend on email or memory. SAP tracks who must act, sends reminders, and logs everything.
  • 📦 GR example: a 101 GR above 10,000 units is posted → the quality manager gets a work item "Check GR 5000001234" in their inbox → approves or rejects.
  • 🛒 Classic real use: PO release / approval, invoice approval, leave requests.

🧱 Building blocks

BlockT-codeMeaning
📦 Business objectSWO1 (BOR) or class with IF_WORKFLOWThe "thing" + its events and methods. BUS2017 = goods movement, BUS2012 = PO
🔔 Event—"Something happened", e.g. BUS2017.CREATED
📝 TaskPFTCOne step of work (TS…), e.g. "display GR"
🗺️ Workflow templateSWDDThe flow (WS…): steps, decisions, conditions
👤 AgentRule / position / userWho gets the work item
📥 InboxSBWPBusiness Workplace: where users see work items (or Fiori My Inbox)
🧠 Remember: object (BUS2017) → event → workflow (SWDD, WS) → tasks (PFTC, TS) → agent → inbox (SBWP).

🛠️ Create a basic workflow

  1. ⚙️ SWU3: check workflow customizing is done (green ticks). Basis / workflow admin does this once.
  2. 📦 Find the object and event: SWO1 → BUS2017 → events → CREATED. Check the event is raised: SWELS switch on event trace, post a GR, read SWEL.
  3. 🗺️ SWDD: new workflow. In the Basic data → Start events, add BUS2017 CREATED. Container element for the GR object.
  4. ➕ Add steps: Condition (quantity > 10,000) → User decision "Approve GR?" (Approve / Reject) → Send mail for the result.
  5. 👤 Agents for the decision: a position (quality manager) or a rule. Binding: pass the GR object from the workflow container into each step.
  6. ✅ Save → Activate → SAP gives a number like WS90000012. Activate the event linkage (SWE2 shows it).
  7. 🧪 Test: SWUS (start with a test GR) or post a real 101 GR → check the agent's SBWP inbox.
☁️ S/4HANA today: many approvals use flexible workflow (Fiori app "Manage Workflows", configured by key users, e.g. PO approval). Cross-system processes: SAP Build Process Automation on BTP.
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q59)

"SAP Business Workflow automates processes with people steps: an event on a business object starts a workflow that sends work items to the right agents' inboxes and reacts to their decisions. To build one I check SWU3, find the object and event, for example BUS2017 CREATED for goods movements, design the template in SWDD with steps like a condition and a user decision, assign agents through positions or rules, bind the object through the container, activate the event linkage and test with SWUS and SBWP. I built a GR approval for big 101 quantities. In S/4 I'd first check flexible workflow."

🧠 Remember: SWU3 → event (SWELS/SWEL) → SWDD steps → agents + binding → activate (SWE2) → test SWUS → SBWP.

🔍 Monitor and fix workflows

T-codeUse
SBWPUser's inbox (work items)
SWI1Find work items by date, task, status
SWI2_FREQWork items by frequency (how many per task)
SWIAAdmin: execute / forward / complete work items for others
SWPRRestart workflows that ended in error
SWELS / SWELSwitch on / read the event trace (was the event raised? did it start the workflow?)
SWE2Event linkage: which event starts which workflow, active or not

🚨 Common problems

  • ❌ Workflow didn't start: event not raised (SWEL empty) or linkage inactive (SWE2).
  • 👻 Nobody got the work item: agent rule returned no one, or the task is not "general" / not assigned to that user.
  • 🔴 Status ERROR: read the workflow log (SWI1 → work item → technical log), fix the cause, restart in SWPR.
🧠 Remember: didn't start → SWEL + SWE2. No agent → rule / task assignment. Error → log + SWPR.

😂 OData & workflow tricks

  • 🍽️ MPC = Menu, DPC = Dish: Model Provider = what's on the menu ($metadata), Data Provider = cooks the food (data).
  • 🧩 Only touch _EXT: "EXT = mera EXTension, baaki SAP ka" (the rest is regenerated).
  • 🔁 CRUD = GET-POST-PUT-DELETE: "Get, Post, Put, Delete = Gaadi Parking Pe Daalo" 🚗.
  • 🛡️ CSRF token = entry pass: GET gives you the pass ("Fetch"), POST needs it at the gate. No pass = 403.
  • 🔴 External breakpoint: "bahar se call aaya, bahar ka breakpoint lagao" (call comes from outside → external breakpoint).
  • 📥 SBWP = "SAP Box With Pending work" = the inbox.
  • 🧾 WS = WorkflowS template, TS = TaSk. BUS2017 = goods movement ("20-17 GR ka saal").

🎮 Your turn (answer first, then tap)

🎤 FULL MODULE ANSWER

"OData exposes SAP data over HTTP as JSON for Fiori and external apps. Classically I build it in SEGW: model from a DDIC structure, generate MPC and DPC, code CRUD in DPC_EXT methods, register in /IWFND/MAINT_SERVICE and test in /IWFND/GW_CLIENT; in S/4 and BTP I use RAP with CDS, a service definition and binding. CRUD maps to GET, POST, PUT or PATCH and DELETE, changes need a CSRF token, and I debug with external breakpoints plus the IWFND and IWBEP error logs. SAP Workflow automates approvals: an event like BUS2017 CREATED starts a SWDD workflow that sends work items to agents' SBWP inboxes, monitored with SWI1, SWEL and SWPR."

DOHands-on

✅ BTP is great for OData (RAP). ❌ No SEGW and no SAP Business Workflow there.

  1. Use your ZI_GR_ITEM CDS view from Module 10. Create ZC_GR_ITEM on top with @UI.lineItem annotations.
  2. New → Service Definition ZUI_GR: expose ZC_GR_Item as GrItem;
  3. New → Service Binding → OData V4 – UI → Activate → Publish.
  4. Click Preview on GrItem → a Fiori list opens. Add ?$top=5 style options in the service URL to see JSON.
  1. /IWFND/MAINT_SERVICE: look at an active service and its system alias. Test it in /IWFND/GW_CLIENT with $metadata.
  2. Sandbox: SEGW project ZGR, import a Z structure, generate, redefine GRITEMSET_GET_ENTITYSET with the L3 code. Register and test.
  3. Set an external breakpoint in it, call again, see the debugger. Make an error on purpose and find it in /IWBEP/ERROR_LOG.
  4. Workflow: SWELS on for your user → post a test GR → SWEL: is BUS2017 CREATED there? Open SWDD and look at any existing WS… template.

TESTQuick check

1. In SEGW, where do you put your data code?

Non-EXT classes are regenerated. MPC = model, DPC = data.

2. T-code to register an OData service?

GW_CLIENT = test. SWDD = workflow builder.

3. Which HTTP method creates an entity?

POST → CREATE_ENTITY. PUT/PATCH update.

4. POST returns 403 Forbidden. Most common cause?

Fetch the token with GET, send it with the change request.

5. Breakpoint for OData calls must be…

HTTP requests don't run in your GUI session.

6. A workflow didn't start after GR. First check?

Was the event raised, and is the linkage active?
Answer all 6 first.

🌐 Part 1 · OData service (Q60)

  • OData = REST over HTTP, JSON / XML, for Fiori / mobile / external. SAP Gateway (/IWFND hub, /IWBEP backend).
  • URL: /sap/opu/odata/sap/ZGR_SRV/GrItemSet?$filter=… · $metadata.
  • SEGW: model (import DDIC) → generate MPC / DPC (+ _EXT) → code in DPC_EXT → register /IWFND/MAINT_SERVICE → test /IWFND/GW_CLIENT.
  • GET_ENTITYSET → et_entityset (+ get_osql_where_clause_convert). GET_ENTITY → it_key_tab → er_entity.
  • RAP: CDS → service definition (expose) → service binding V2 / V4 → publish → preview. BTP = RAP only.

🔁 Part 2 · CRUD (Q61)

GET (ENTITYSET / ENTITY) · POST (CREATE_ENTITY) · PUT / PATCH (UPDATE_ENTITY) · DELETE (DELETE_ENTITY) · deep insert · $batch · function import. Options: $filter $select $top $skip $orderby $expand $inlinecount. Changes need X-CSRF-Token (else 403).

🐞 Part 3 · Debug (Q62)

External breakpoint (calling user) in DPC_EXT → GW_CLIENT → /IWFND/ERROR_LOG, /IWBEP/ERROR_LOG, /IWFND/TRACES → CACHE_CLEANUP (IWFND + IWBEP) → F12 network. 400 bad request · 403 auth / CSRF · 404 not found · 500 dump.

🗺️ Part 4 · Workflow (Q59)

  • Event on business object (BUS2017 GR, BUS2012 PO; SWO1 / IF_WORKFLOW) starts a workflow (SWDD, WS…) of tasks (PFTC, TS…) → agents → inbox SBWP.
  • Build: SWU3 → SWELS / SWEL → SWDD (start event, condition, user decision, mail) → agents + binding → activate → SWE2 → test SWUS.
  • Monitor: SWI1, SWIA, SWPR (restart errors), SWEL. S/4: flexible workflow. BTP: SAP Build Process Automation.
🪤 Traps: code in DPC (not EXT) → lost. Session breakpoint → never stops. No CSRF → 403. Model change not visible → cache cleanup. Workflow not started → SWEL / SWE2.
🎤 30-SECOND ANSWER

"OData exposes SAP data over HTTP: in SEGW I model, generate MPC and DPC, code CRUD in DPC_EXT, register in /IWFND/MAINT_SERVICE and test in GW_CLIENT, or in S/4 and BTP I use RAP with a CDS service binding. I debug with external breakpoints and the IWFND / IWBEP error logs. Workflow automates approvals: BUS2017 CREATED starts a SWDD workflow that sends work items to SBWP inboxes."

MODULE 12 · PDF Q63, Q65–67 (Q64 in IDoc tab) · COMPLETE ✅

Jobs, LUW, Parallel, Transport

Run programs at night, save data all-or-nothing, split big work, and move code from DEV to PRD. T-codes: SM36, SM37, SM13, SE09, STMS.

😫 PROBLEMA 2-hour GR report blocks a user's screen. Half-saved data after an error. One job is too slow. Code works in DEV but must reach PRD safely.
💡 IDEABackground jobs for long runs, one SAP LUW for all-or-nothing saves, many parallel tasks for speed, transport requests to move code.
🔧 HOWSM36 / SM37 · CALL FUNCTION … IN UPDATE TASK + COMMIT WORK · STARTING NEW TASK · SE09 + STMS.
➡️ NEXT🎉 All modules done. Use the Revise tab daily.

LEARNLevel by level

Part 1 · Background jobs (Q63) Part 2 · LUW (Q65) Part 3 · Parallel processing (Q66) Part 4 · Transport (Q67) Part 5 · Wrap-up
PART 1 · JOBS

🤔 What is a background job? (Q63)

A background job runs a program without a user screen, on a background work process, at a time you choose. The user can close SAP GUI and go home.

  • ❓ Why? Long reports, mass updates, nightly interfaces. Dialog programs are stopped after a time limit (often 10 min, TIME_OUT dump). Background has no such limit.
  • 📦 GR example: every night at 2 AM run ZGR_ALV for yesterday's 101 GRs, send the list to spool / email.

🛠️ Create a job in SM36

  1. 📝 First save a variant (fixed selection values): SE38 → program → Goto → Variants, e.g. YESTERDAY (dynamic date = today − 1).
  2. SM36 → job name Z_GR_DAILY → Step: ABAP program ZGR_ALV + variant YESTERDAY (+ printer / spool settings).
  3. Start condition: Immediate · Date/Time (2:00) · After job · After event (SM62) · Periodic (daily).
  4. Save → the job is Released.

👀 Monitor in SM37

StatusMeaning
ScheduledSaved, no start condition yet
ReleasedWaiting for its start time
ReadyStart time reached, waiting for a free background process
ActiveRunning now
Finished ✅Done. Output in Spool (SP01)
Canceled ❌Error: read the job log, then ST22 for the dump
🧠 Remember: variant → SM36 (step + start condition) → SM37 (status, job log, spool).

🔧 Schedule a job from ABAP

DATA: lv_job TYPE tbtcjob-jobname VALUE 'Z_GR_DAILY',
      lv_cnt TYPE tbtcjob-jobcount.
CALL FUNCTION 'JOB_OPEN'
  EXPORTING jobname = lv_job  IMPORTING jobcount = lv_cnt.
SUBMIT zgr_alv USING SELECTION-SET 'YESTERDAY'
       VIA JOB lv_job NUMBER lv_cnt AND RETURN.
CALL FUNCTION 'JOB_CLOSE'
  EXPORTING jobname = lv_job  jobcount = lv_cnt  strtimmed = 'X'.

🚶 What each line means

  • JOB_OPEN = create the job, get its unique job count number.
  • SUBMIT … VIA JOB … AND RETURN = add the report as a step, with variant YESTERDAY, and come back.
  • JOB_CLOSE … strtimmed = 'X' = release it to start immediately. (Or pass a date / time.)

📏 Rules inside a background program

  • 🔍 sy-batch = 'X' when running in background. Use it to skip popups.
  • 🚫 No front-end: no GUI_UPLOAD / GUI_DOWNLOAD (no PC!), no popups. Use application server files: OPEN DATASET, view in AL11.
  • 📝 MESSAGE … TYPE 'S' / 'I' go to the job log. Type E / A cancel the job.
  • 🖨️ WRITE / ALV output goes to the spool.

🐞 Debug a job

Running: SM50 → select the process → Program → Debugging. Finished / canceled: in SM37 select the job, type JDBG in the command field → it re-runs in the debugger (in dialog).

🎤 INTERVIEW (Q63)

"I schedule background jobs in SM36 with a step of program plus variant and a start condition like a date, periodic run, after another job or an event, and monitor them in SM37 with job log and spool. From code I use JOB_OPEN, SUBMIT VIA JOB AND RETURN, and JOB_CLOSE. Background programs check sy-batch, can't use GUI_UPLOAD or popups, so files go to the application server with OPEN DATASET. Our nightly GR job once canceled because it called GUI_DOWNLOAD; I switched to OPEN DATASET and debugged the old run with JDBG in SM37."

🧠 Remember: JOB_OPEN → SUBMIT VIA JOB → JOB_CLOSE. sy-batch. No GUI_* in background. JDBG.
PART 2 · LUW

🤔 What is an LUW? (Q65)

LUW = Logical Unit of Work: a group of changes that must happen all together or not at all.

📦
A GR posting (MIGO 101) writes MKPF, MSEG (or MATDOC), stock tables, the PO history EKBE and an FI document. If FI fails, the stock must not change either. That's one LUW.

🆚 Two kinds

🗄️ Database LUW🏢 SAP LUW
WhatChanges between two DB commitsA business transaction over several screens
Who controlsThe databaseSAP (ABAP)
LengthOne dialog step (one screen)Many dialog steps until COMMIT WORK
Ends withImplicit commit (screen change, RFC call, WAIT) or COMMIT WORKCOMMIT WORK (or ROLLBACK WORK)

🧩 The problem: every screen change makes an implicit DB commit. If you UPDATE a table on screen 1 and something fails on screen 3, screen 1's change is already saved. 😱

💡 The fix: don't write to the DB on each screen. Collect all changes and write them together at the end, in one DB LUW. Tools: update task (L4), PERFORM … ON COMMIT, and SAP locks (SM12, Module 2) to protect the data meanwhile.

🧠 Remember: DB LUW = one screen step. SAP LUW = whole business transaction, bundled until COMMIT WORK.

🔧 Bundling with the update task

CALL FUNCTION 'Z_GR_LOG_UPDATE' IN UPDATE TASK
  EXPORTING is_log = ls_log.       " only registered, not run yet
CALL FUNCTION 'Z_GR_STATUS_UPDATE' IN UPDATE TASK
  EXPORTING iv_mblnr = lv_mblnr.
" ... more screens, more checks ...
COMMIT WORK AND WAIT.   " now both run together: all or nothing

🚶 Step by step

  1. IN UPDATE TASK doesn't run the FM now. SAP writes the call and its data into a queue (VB tables).
  2. At COMMIT WORK, a special update work process runs all queued FMs in one DB LUW.
  3. Any error inside → everything rolls back. The entry stays in SM13 with status error, and the user gets an express mail.
  4. AND WAIT = wait until the update finished (synchronous). Without it the program goes on immediately.
TermMeaning
Update moduleFM with processing type "Update module" in SE37 Attributes. No EXPORTING params, no COMMIT inside
V1Critical, time-sensitive (stock, documents). Runs first, can't be lost
V2Less critical (statistics). Runs after V1
PERFORM x ON COMMITRun a FORM at COMMIT WORK (in your own process)
ROLLBACK WORKThrow away all queued changes
BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMITCommit after BAPIs (WAIT = 'X'). BAPIs never commit themselves
🪤 Traps: 1) COMMIT WORK inside an update module, BADI or user exit breaks SAP's LUW → never do it. 2) Reading the data right after COMMIT WORK without AND WAIT may not find it yet.
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q65)

"A database LUW is the set of changes between two database commits, usually one dialog step, because screen changes and RFC calls commit implicitly. An SAP LUW spans the whole business transaction across screens, so changes are bundled with CALL FUNCTION IN UPDATE TASK or PERFORM ON COMMIT and executed together at COMMIT WORK, all or nothing; ROLLBACK WORK discards them. Update modules run as V1 for critical data or V2 for statistics, and failed updates show in SM13. In my GR program I logged and updated status through update FMs; reading the log right after commit found nothing until I used COMMIT WORK AND WAIT."

🧠 Remember: IN UPDATE TASK = queue. COMMIT WORK = run all. Error → rollback + SM13. Never COMMIT inside exits.
PART 3 · PARALLEL

🤔 What is parallel processing? (Q66)

Split a big job into packages and run them at the same time in several work processes, instead of one after another.

📦
GR totals for 40 plants: one by one = 40 × 3 min = 2 hours. 8 in parallel = about 15 minutes.
LOOP AT lt_plants INTO DATA(lv_werks).
  CALL FUNCTION 'Z_GR_TOTAL_PLANT'      " must be RFC-enabled
    STARTING NEW TASK |GR{ lv_werks }|
    DESTINATION IN GROUP DEFAULT
    CALLING lcl_par=>on_done ON END OF TASK
    EXPORTING  iv_werks = lv_werks
    EXCEPTIONS resource_failure = 1 OTHERS = 2.
  IF sy-subrc = 0. gv_sent = gv_sent + 1. ENDIF.
ENDLOOP.
WAIT FOR ASYNCHRONOUS TASKS UNTIL gv_done >= gv_sent.
METHOD on_done.                      " IMPORTING p_task TYPE clike
  RECEIVE RESULTS FROM FUNCTION 'Z_GR_TOTAL_PLANT'
    IMPORTING ev_total = lv_total.
  APPEND lv_total TO gt_totals.   gv_done = gv_done + 1.
ENDMETHOD.

🚶 Step by step

  1. STARTING NEW TASK 'GR1000' = start the FM in a new work process and don't wait (asynchronous RFC, aRFC). Task name must be unique.
  2. DESTINATION IN GROUP DEFAULT = use the server group's free dialog processes (groups in RZ12).
  3. CALLING … ON END OF TASK = when this task finishes, SAP calls on_done.
  4. RECEIVE RESULTS = collect that task's output. Count finished tasks.
  5. WAIT FOR ASYNCHRONOUS TASKS UNTIL … = the main program waits until all tasks reported back.
🧠 Remember: STARTING NEW TASK + IN GROUP + CALLING ON END OF TASK → RECEIVE RESULTS → WAIT UNTIL.

📏 Rules and other options

  • 🔌 The FM must be remote-enabled (SE37 Attributes). Parameters by value.
  • 🧮 Don't take all work processes: limit the number of running tasks (e.g. max 8), or check free ones with SPBT_INITIALIZE. Handle RESOURCE_FAILURE: wait and retry.
  • 📦 Packages must be independent (no two tasks updating the same rows) to avoid lock errors.
  • 🔒 Each task has its own LUW: commit inside each task, not once at the end.
OptionHowWhen
⚡ aRFCSTARTING NEW TASKResults needed back in one program
📅 Several jobsSame report, variants per plant range, scheduled togetherSimple split, no code change
🧰 CL_ABAP_PARALLELClass that runs packages in parallelNewer releases, less code
📨 bgRFCBackground RFC queuesGuaranteed, ordered async processing
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q66)

"I split the data into independent packages and call an RFC-enabled function module with STARTING NEW TASK, DESTINATION IN GROUP DEFAULT and a CALLING ON END OF TASK callback, collect output with RECEIVE RESULTS, and WAIT FOR ASYNCHRONOUS TASKS until all have returned. I limit parallel tasks so users keep free work processes and retry on RESOURCE_FAILURE. Simpler options are several background jobs with different variants, or CL_ABAP_PARALLEL. For GR totals across 40 plants this cut runtime from about 2 hours to 15 minutes; at first it failed with resource errors until I capped tasks at 8."

🧠 Remember: RFC-enabled FM, independent packages, cap the tasks, handle RESOURCE_FAILURE.
PART 4 · TRANSPORT

🚚 The system landscape (Q67)

🧪 DEV (build)→✅ QAS (test)→🏭 PRD (live)

You never code in PRD. You build in DEV and move the objects with a transport request (TR) through the CTS (Change and Transport System).

📦 Transport request basics

  • 🧾 A request (e.g. DEVK900123) holds one or more tasks, one per developer. Objects you change are recorded in your task.
  • 🗂️ Objects must be in a real package (SE21, e.g. ZGR). Package $TMP = local, can't be transported.
  • 🔒 While in a request, the object is locked for other requests.
TypeCarriesClient
🛠️ Workbench requestRepository objects: programs, classes, tables, CDS, SmartFormsCross-client
⚙️ Customizing requestSPRO settings, table entries (e.g. SM30 config)Client-specific
📄 Transport of copiesCopy of objects to test in QAS without releasing the original (SE01)—
🧠 Remember: DEV → QAS → PRD. Request has tasks. Workbench = code (cross-client), Customizing = config (per client). $TMP = no transport.

🛠️ Steps: from DEV to PRD

  1. 🆕 Save your object in package ZGR → SAP asks for a request → create one in SE09 / SE10 (or pick yours).
  2. 🔍 Before release: syntax check, ATC check, activate everything, check object list (all pieces inside? data element, table, program, text elements).
  3. 🔓 SE09: release your task first, then the request. Release = export: files (data + cofile) are written to /usr/sap/trans.
  4. 📥 Basis (or you, if allowed) imports in STMS → import queue of QAS → Import.
  5. 🧪 Business tests in QAS. OK? Same request is imported to PRD (from the PRD import queue), usually in a change window.

🚦 Import return codes

RCMeaning
0✅ Success
4🟡 Warning (usually OK, read the log)
8🔴 Error: e.g. syntax error, missing object (a data element in another request not yet imported)
12+⛔ Abort (system problem)
🪤 Traps: 1) Order matters: import the request with the table / data element before the program that uses it. 2) Forgot text elements or a message class → RC 4/8 or missing texts in QAS. 3) Never import a request to PRD that wasn't tested in QAS. 4) SCC1 copies customizing between clients of the same system; it's not a transport to another system.
🎤 INTERVIEW (Q67)

"We have a DEV, QAS, PRD landscape managed by CTS. I save objects in a Z package so they're recorded in a workbench request; customizing goes into customizing requests. Before release I run syntax and ATC checks and verify the object list, then release my task and the request in SE09, which exports it, and Basis imports it through STMS into QAS for testing and later into PRD. I check the return codes: 0 or 4 is fine, 8 means errors. Once my GR report failed in QAS with RC 8 because its new data element was in a colleague's unreleased request, so we released and imported that request first."

🧠 Remember: package → request (SE09) → checks → release task, then request → STMS import QAS → test → PRD. RC 0/4 ok, 8 error.

😂 Module tricks

  • 🌙 SM36 = "Schedule Me", SM37 = "Show Me": 6 creates, 7 watches.
  • 🐞 JDBG = "Job DeBuG": type it on a finished job in SM37.
  • 🖥️ sy-batch = 'X' = "PC nahi hai, popup mat dikhao" (no PC, no popup).
  • 🛒 SAP LUW = shopping cart: add items (IN UPDATE TASK), pay once (COMMIT WORK). Card declined = nothing bought (rollback).
  • 🥇🥈 V1 = VIP (stock, documents), V2 = Very later (statistics).
  • 🧑‍🍳 Parallel = many cooks: STARTING NEW TASK = hire a cook, RECEIVE RESULTS = collect the dish, WAIT = wait for all dishes. Too many cooks = no kitchen left (RESOURCE_FAILURE).
  • 🚚 DEV → QAS → PRD = "Dekho, Aazmao, Phir Release" (build, test, release).
  • 🚦 RC 0-4-8: "0 = party, 4 = theek hai, 8 = gadbad".
📨 Q64 (IDoc) belongs to this module in the PDF. It's fully covered in the IDoc tab (10 levels).

🎮 Your turn (answer first, then tap)

🎤 FULL MODULE ANSWER

"Long programs run as background jobs scheduled in SM36 or with JOB_OPEN, SUBMIT VIA JOB and JOB_CLOSE, and monitored in SM37 with job log and spool. Data consistency comes from the SAP LUW: changes are queued with CALL FUNCTION IN UPDATE TASK and written together at COMMIT WORK, with failures in SM13. For speed I split work into independent packages and run an RFC-enabled FM with STARTING NEW TASK, collecting results with RECEIVE RESULTS. Code moves from DEV to QAS to PRD in workbench or customizing requests released in SE09 and imported with STMS, checking return codes."

DOHands-on

⚠️ Mostly not in the BTP trial: no SM36 / SM37, no STMS. Cloud uses Application Jobs (Fiori) and gCTS / software components for transport. COMMIT WORK is handled by RAP for you.

  1. Look at ADT: your objects are in a software component / package. That is the cloud idea of a transport.
  2. Read the L4 LUW code and explain it out loud: queue → commit → all or nothing.
  1. SE38: save a variant for your GR ALV. SM36: job with that variant, start immediately. SM37: watch status, open spool.
  2. Add IF sy-batch = 'X' logic and a MESSAGE … TYPE 'S'. Find the message in the job log.
  3. SM13: look at the update requests list (filter on your user / today). SM12: see locks while editing a PO.
  4. SE09: open your requests, look at tasks and object lists. Don't release anything you don't own.
  5. STMS → import overview (display only): see the QAS queue and return codes of past imports.

TESTQuick check

1. T-code to monitor background jobs?

SM36 = define, SM37 = monitor, SM13 = update requests.

2. Which won't work in a background job?

No front-end PC in background. Use application server files.

3. When does a FM called IN UPDATE TASK run?

It is queued and executed by the update process at COMMIT WORK.

4. Where do you see failed update requests?

SM13 shows update errors (with express mail to the user).

5. Parallel processing with aRFC needs an FM that is…

STARTING NEW TASK calls it via RFC.

6. Programs, classes and tables travel in a…

Customizing = client-specific settings. $TMP is never transported.
Answer all 6 first.
⚡ Quick revise. Read top to bottom in 2 minutes. Forgot something? Switch to 📖 Learn.

🌙 Part 1 · Jobs (Q63)

  • Variant → SM36 (step: program + variant; start: immediate / date / after job / event / periodic) → SM37 (scheduled, released, ready, active, finished, canceled) → job log, spool SP01, dump ST22.
  • Code: JOB_OPEN → SUBMIT … VIA JOB … NUMBER … AND RETURN → JOB_CLOSE (strtimmed).
  • sy-batch, no GUI_UPLOAD / popups → OPEN DATASET (AL11). Debug: SM50 or JDBG in SM37.

🛒 Part 2 · LUW (Q65)

  • DB LUW = between DB commits (one dialog step; implicit commit on screen change, RFC, WAIT). SAP LUW = whole business transaction until COMMIT WORK.
  • CALL FUNCTION … IN UPDATE TASK (queue) → COMMIT WORK [AND WAIT] → update process, all or nothing · ROLLBACK WORK · PERFORM … ON COMMIT.
  • Update module V1 critical / V2 statistics · errors in SM13 · BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT · never COMMIT in exits.

🧑‍🍳 Part 3 · Parallel (Q66)

STARTING NEW TASK + DESTINATION IN GROUP DEFAULT (RZ12) + CALLING … ON END OF TASK → RECEIVE RESULTS → WAIT FOR ASYNCHRONOUS TASKS UNTIL. RFC-enabled FM, independent packages, cap tasks, RESOURCE_FAILURE retry. Options: several jobs, CL_ABAP_PARALLEL, bgRFC.

🚚 Part 4 · Transport (Q67)

  • DEV → QAS → PRD via CTS. Request (DEVK9…) with tasks. Package needed ($TMP not transportable).
  • Workbench (repository, cross-client) · customizing (client-specific) · transport of copies (SE01).
  • SE09: checks → release task → release request (export to /usr/sap/trans) → STMS import QAS → test → PRD. RC 0 ok, 4 warning, 8 error, 12 abort. Order matters. SCC1 = client copy in same system.
🪤 Traps: GUI_* in background. Reading data after COMMIT WORK without AND WAIT. COMMIT in a BADI. Too many parallel tasks. Dependent object in another request → RC 8.
🎤 30-SECOND ANSWER

"I run long reports as SM36 jobs with variants and monitor them in SM37. I keep data consistent with the SAP LUW: update-task FMs queued and executed together at COMMIT WORK, errors in SM13. For speed I use aRFC with STARTING NEW TASK and RECEIVE RESULTS. Code goes DEV → QAS → PRD in workbench requests released in SE09 and imported with STMS."

MY PROJECT · B2B WHOLESALE (METRO) · ECC ON HANA · 4.5 YEARS

My project story

Your intro, your real flow, your real tickets, and how to talk about them in standard SAP words.

PART 1 · INTRO

🎤 30-second intro

⏱️ 30 SECONDS

"I'm an SAP ABAP developer and support consultant with about 4.5 years on ECC on HANA, for a large B2B wholesale client. I worked on a custom procure-to-pay integration: importing goods receipt files and XML invoices from the application server, matching them in a custom three-way match, moving them through approval and creating posting files for FI. I was a senior in a 13-member team, guiding 3 to 4 people, and I owned production support, enhancements to these programs like e-invoicing, and transports. I'm now strengthening modern ABAP: OOP, CDS and RAP."

🎤 90-second intro

⏱️ 90 SECONDS

"I'm an SAP ABAP developer and support consultant with about 4.5 years of experience on ECC on HANA, for a large B2B wholesale client. I worked in a 13-member team as a senior and guided 3 to 4 colleagues. My main area was a custom procure-to-pay integration. Goods receipt flat files and XML invoices arrived on the application server in AL11. Custom import programs, running as background jobs, validated them, including the check record, and loaded them into staging tables. A matching program then did a three-way match on invoice number, order number, amount and supplier, moved documents through approval statuses, and a posting program balanced the transactions and created posting files for the FI interfaces. I also handled vendor master loads through a CVI-based vendor API into LFA1 and LFB1, and system setup like business partners, GL accounts and tax codes in FTXP. I built changes too: for example e-invoicing, where invoices starting with 5 were flagged and government details were stored in new tables, and a VAT percentage and header-level check for Pakistan before posting. I did transports and retrofits across two development and test lines. Now I'm strengthening modern ABAP: OOP, CDS views and RAP on BTP, and I practice standard BADIs and BAPIs in a sandbox."

  • 🗣️ Say it slowly. Stop after "approval statuses" and breathe.
  • 🎯 Every sentence is a door: the interviewer will pick one (AL11? three-way match? e-invoice?). All doors are prepared in L4–L11.
🧠 Remember the intro order: Who (4.5 yrs, senior) → What (F-I-M-A-P) → Build (e-invoice, VAT) → Now (modern ABAP).

PART 2 · THE FLOW

🗺️ F-I-M-A-P: the big picture

📂 F · GR flat file + XML invoice in AL11→📥 I · Y import programs → Y staging tables→🔗 M · Referencing / 3-way match → status→✅ A · Approval (by status)→💰 P · Posting program balances → posting file → FI interfaces

Side flows: vendor master (file → Y table → CVI vendor API → LFA1 / LFB1), debit / return notes sent to FI, system setup (BP, GL, tax codes).

🔗 Each step = one module in this app (tap to open)

StepInterview topicOpen
📂 Files in AL11Application server files, OPEN DATASET, background jobs
📥 Y staging tablesDDIC tables, keys, lock objects
👤 Vendor APIBAPI vs BDC, calling FMs / classes
🔗 Matching big tablesHashed tables, FAE, performance

💰 Posting all-or-nothingLUW, COMMIT WORK
📨 File interface vs IDocWhy IDoc / ALE is the standard way

🧠 Remember: F-I-M-A-P. Every letter has a module behind it, so every answer can jump to theory.

📂 F · Files in AL11

AL11 shows folders on the application server (not your PC). Sender systems drop files there. Background jobs read them. Upload / download a file by hand: CG3Z (PC → server), CG3Y (server → PC).

📄 GR flat file shape

RecordHolds
H headerSupplier, Metro order no., GR date
I itemsArticle, quantity, amount (many lines)
T check record (trailer)Number of items + total amount, to prove the file is complete

🔧 How such a file is read (example code)

OPEN DATASET lv_path FOR INPUT IN TEXT MODE ENCODING DEFAULT.
IF sy-subrc <> 0.
  MESSAGE |File not found: { lv_path }| TYPE 'E'.  " wrong path / variant → job cancels
ENDIF.
DO.
  READ DATASET lv_path INTO lv_line.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. EXIT. ENDIF.             " end of file
  CASE lv_line(1).                               " first character = record type
    WHEN 'I'. lv_sum = lv_sum + lv_amount. lv_cnt = lv_cnt + 1.
    WHEN 'T'. " compare lv_sum / lv_cnt with the check record
  ENDCASE.
ENDDO.
CLOSE DATASET lv_path.

🧾 XML invoice (one common way)

TRY.
    CALL TRANSFORMATION zinv_xml SOURCE XML lv_xml RESULT invoice = ls_inv.
  CATCH cx_transformation_error INTO DATA(lx).
    " e.g. letters in an amount tag → log lx->get_text( ), set error status
ENDTRY.

🔑 CALL TRANSFORMATION maps XML tags to an ABAP structure (transformation made in STRANS). A wrong type in a tag raises an exception. Your project's exact code may differ: say "the import program parsed the XML into a structure".

🧠 Remember: AL11 = server folder. OPEN → READ loop → CLOSE. H-I-T file. Check record = "bill ka total".

📥 I · Staging tables and vendor master

🗄️ Why Y staging tables?

  • Raw file data is first stored as it came, with a status. Nothing is lost if a later step fails.
  • Support can see and reprocess one record without asking for the file again.
  • Y (or Z) = customer namespace. Both mean custom objects.

👤 Vendor master load

Vendor file in AL11→Y program fills Y staging table→CVI vendor create API→LFA1 (general) · LFB1 (company code) · related tables
  • 🧩 CVI = Customer Vendor Integration: keeps the Business Partner (BP) and the classic vendor (LFA1 …) in sync. In S/4HANA, BP is the only way.
  • 🗂️ LFA1 = vendor general data (LieFerant, Allgemein). LFB1 = per company code (Buchungskreis).
  • 🧰 Interview words: "I used SAP's vendor API, wrapped in a custom program, instead of BDC on XK01 — faster and upgrade-safe." (Module 8: BAPI vs BDC.)

⚙️ System setup you did

SetupT-code
Business partnerBP
GL accountsFS00
Tax codesFTXP
⚠️ Honest: say "I maintained them as per the functional spec / process" if asked why. You know how, the FI consultant decides what.
🧠 Remember: staging = safe copy + status. CVI = BP ↔ vendor sync. LFA1 general, LFB1 company code. FTXP tax codes.

🔗 M · Referencing = custom 3-way match

The referencing program links an invoice to its GR and order. If the data agrees, the status moves forward. Standard SAP does the same in MIRO (PO ↔ GR ↔ invoice).

Match onWhy
Invoice numberSame document, no duplicates
Metro order numberInvoice belongs to the right order
Supplier / BPRight vendor
AmountBilled = received

🚦 Status-driven processing (example names, use your real ones)

10 Imported→20 Validated→30 Matched→40 Approved→50 Sent to posting·E… error statuses

Each program picks only its input status and writes the next one. Like IDoc statuses (51 / 53) in the IDoc tab. A record "stuck" in a status = the next job didn't pick it or rejected it.

🏎️ Matching fast (interview bonus)

Thousands of invoices × thousands of GRs: read both sets once, put GRs in a hashed / sorted table keyed by order + invoice no., then READ TABLE … WITH TABLE KEY. No SELECT inside the loop (Perf tab).

🧠 Remember: referencing = custom 3-way match (standard: MIRO). Status machine: each job picks one status, writes the next.

✅ A · Approval & 💰 P · Posting

  • ✅ Approval: for certain statuses an approval step changes the status. Only approved statuses go further.
  • ⚖️ Posting program: picks approved records, balances the transaction (debits = credits, invoice vs GR amounts, statuses), then writes a posting flat file into AL11.
  • 📤 FI posting interfaces pick the file from AL11 and send it to the FI side, which posts the accounting document.
  • 🧾 Debit notes / return notes are also created by the programs and sent to FI the same way.

🌉 Same thing in standard words

Your stepStandard SAP
Approval by statusSAP Business Workflow / release strategy
Posting file to FIBAPI_ACC_DOCUMENT_POST or an IDoc / interface
Balancing checkFI checks debit = credit before posting
Debit note / return noteMIRO subsequent debit / credit memo; return to vendor = movement type 122
🧠 Remember: approve by status → post = balance → file → FI interface. Standard: workflow + accounting BAPI.
PART 3 · STORY 1

🌍 Story: Pakistan VAT and header check (your best story)

STARWhat happened
🟦 SituationFI rejected some postings as not balanced. Our program only compared the sum of line items (invoice vs GR). For Pakistan, invoices use a VAT percentage per item. The old system checked it, but the new one had no such logic, so wrong documents still went to posting.
🟨 TaskFind why unbalanced documents passed, and stop them before posting.
🟩 ActionDebugged the posting run, compared item and header amounts, found the missing check. Added for Pakistan: a header total vs item total check and a VAT % recalculation per item. Mismatch → error status, not posting. Tested with good and bad files, transported, retrofitted to the second line.
🟥 ResultNo more unbalanced Pakistan postings reaching FI. Errors were caught in our system with a clear status, so support could fix them before FI.

🔧 The idea in code (example, not the client code)

IF ls_inv-land1 = 'PK'.                        " Pakistan only
  LOOP AT lt_items INTO DATA(ls_it).
    lv_vat = ls_it-net * ls_it-vat_pct / 100.  " recalc VAT
    IF abs( lv_vat - ls_it-vat_amt ) > lc_tolerance.
      lv_error = abap_true.                    " VAT % wrong
    ENDIF.
    lv_total = lv_total + ls_it-gross.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF lv_total <> ls_inv-header_total. lv_error = abap_true. ENDIF.
  IF lv_error = abap_true. ls_inv-status = 'EV'. ENDIF.   " stop: VAT error
ENDIF.

🔑 LAND1 = country key. abs( ) = absolute value. lc_tolerance = small allowed difference for rounding (e.g. 0.01).

💡 Senior touch to mention: "Better design: keep the country list and tolerance in a config table (SM30), not hard-coded 'PK', so new countries need no code change."
🎤 TELL IT (60 s)

"FI was rejecting some Pakistan postings as unbalanced. When I debugged the posting program, I saw it only compared the sum of invoice and GR line items. For Pakistan, items carry a VAT percentage, and the old system checked it, but our new logic didn't, so wrong documents still went to posting. I added a header-total versus item-total check and a VAT recalculation per item for Pakistan, with a small rounding tolerance, setting an error status instead of posting. After testing and transporting it, and retrofitting to the second line, unbalanced postings stopped reaching FI."

🧠 Remember: "PK = Percentage Check". Items-only sum → added header + VAT % check → error status before FI.
PART 3 · STORY 2

🧾 Story: e-invoice change (development story)

STARWhat happened
🟦 SSome countries moved fully to e-invoicing. Invoices now carried government details.
🟨 TChange the invoice import program to recognise e-invoices and store the government data.
🟩 AAdded an indicator: invoice number starting with 5 = e-invoice. Created new Y tables for provider ID, invoice reference number and government ID. Mapped the new XML tags, filled the tables during import, tested, transported and retrofitted.
🟥 RE-invoices were imported with their legal details, ready for matching, posting and audit.
IF ls_inv-invoice_no(1) = '5'.                  " e-invoice indicator
  ls_inv-einvoice = abap_true.
  INSERT yeinv_gov FROM @( VALUE #(               " example table name
    invoice_no  = ls_inv-invoice_no
    provider_id = ls_xml-provider_id
    gov_ref_no  = ls_xml-gov_ref_no ) ).
ENDIF.

🔑 invoice_no(1) = first character (offset / length). INSERT … FROM @( VALUE #( ) ) = insert one row built inline.

🌉 Standard words: "In standard SAP, e-invoicing is handled by SAP Document and Reporting Compliance (eDocument cockpit EDOC_COCKPIT). Ours was a custom solution in the import program."
🎤 TELL IT (45 s)

"When some countries moved to e-invoicing, I changed our invoice import program. Invoices starting with 5 were flagged as e-invoices, and I created new custom tables for the government details like provider ID, invoice reference and government ID, mapped from new XML tags during import. I tested it, transported it and retrofitted it to our second development line. In standard SAP this area is covered by Document and Reporting Compliance."

🧠 Remember: "5 se shuru = e-invoice". Indicator + new Y tables (provider ID, invoice ref, gov ID).
PART 3 · STORY 3

📂 Story: file and XML errors (support story)

ProblemHow you found itFix
Job failed: wrong variant / pathSM37 job log, check the variant values and the AL11 folderCorrect variant / path, rerun the job
Incomplete GR fileItem sum / count ≠ check recordAsk sender to resend the full file, reprocess
XML: text in a number tagDebugged import, conversion failed on that tagSender corrected and resent the file
Mandatory country field missing (e.g. a special text / number for one country)Program check rejected it; analysed which tag was emptyExplained the requirement to the sender; they started sending it
FI says "not balanced"Debug posting balancing (Story 1)Added header + VAT check
🎤 TELL IT (45 s)

"A typical issue was an invoice job failing. I'd check the job log in SM37, the file in AL11 and then debug the import. Once the XML had letters in a numeric amount tag, which failed the conversion; another time a mandatory country-specific field was missing, so the program's validation rejected it. In both cases I identified the exact tag and record, the sender corrected the file, and we reprocessed it. For GR files, I often found incomplete transfers because the item totals didn't match the check record."

🧠 Remember: job log → AL11 file → debug → exact tag / record → sender fixes → reprocess.

🧰 Your support toolkit

ToolUse in your project
SM37Job status, job log, spool of the import / matching / posting jobs
AL11 · CG3Y / CG3ZSee the file · download / upload a file to check or reload
SE38 → VariantsCheck the job's selection values (path, date, country)
SE16NRead Y staging tables: which status is each record in?
Debugger (/h), JDBGStep through import / matching / posting with the real data
ST22Dumps (e.g. conversion errors) with the exact line
SM50A job running too long: what is it doing now?
SE09 / STMSTransports DEV → test, and retrofits

🔁 Transports & retrofit

You had two development + test lines and one production. A change that went live from line 1 had to be retrofitted (re-applied) to line 2, so both lines stay the same and nothing is overwritten later. Direct fixes in production config were also repeated in the other systems.

🎤 TELL IT (30 s)

"We had two development and test lines and one production. After a change went live from one line, I retrofitted it to the other line so both stayed in sync and later transports didn't overwrite it. I released and imported transports and checked the return codes."

🧠 Remember: SM37 → AL11 → variant → SE16N status → debug → ST22. Retrofit = copy the live change to the other line.
PART 4 · BRIDGE

🌉 Your custom piece ↔ standard SAP

Your projectStandard SAP wayWords to use
GR file → Y tablesMIGO 101 / BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE / IDoc MBGMCR → MKPF / MSEG"goods movement BAPI"
XML invoice → Y tablesMIRO / BAPI_INCOMINGINVOICE_CREATE / IDoc INVOIC → RBKP / RSEG"logistics invoice verification"
Referencing (match)MIRO 3-way match with tolerances (OMR6)"three-way match"
Approval statusesWorkflow (SWDD) / release strategy"approval workflow"
Posting file to FIBAPI_ACC_DOCUMENT_POST → BKPF / BSEG"accounting BAPI"
Files in AL11IDoc / ALE, or middleware (PI/PO, CPI) with proxies"inbound interface"
Vendor loadSame idea: BP + CVI API (mandatory in S/4)"master data migration"
E-invoice tablesDocument and Reporting Compliance (EDOC_COCKPIT)"eDocument"
Changing Y programsFor SAP code: BADI / enhancement, not modification"custom vs standard"

🏗️ "If you built it today?"

🎤 ANSWER

"I'd keep the staging and status idea, because it makes support easy, but post standard documents: GRs with BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE, invoices with BAPI_INCOMINGINVOICE_CREATE so MIRO's three-way match and tolerances apply, and FI with BAPI_ACC_DOCUMENT_POST, all inside one LUW with BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT. Files would come through IDocs or middleware instead of AL11, and approvals through workflow. Country rules like the Pakistan VAT check would sit in a config table."

🧠 Remember: keep staging + status, post with BAPIs, IDoc instead of files, workflow for approval, config table for country rules.

🪤 Honest answers to tricky questions

Have you implemented a BADI?"In my project the objects were custom Y programs, so changes were made directly in the code; enhancements are for changing SAP standard. I've implemented a BADI and an implicit enhancement in a sandbox to learn them: [describe L14 practice]." ⚠️ Do the practice first!

📝 About the 🎤 answers in other tabs: many use a practice GR example ("in my GR report…"). In a real interview say "for example…" or "in my sandbox I…", unless it's one of the real stories on this tab.
🧠 Remember: custom = direct code change. Standard = BADI / enhancement. Say what you did + what you practiced.

😂 Project tricks

  • 🗺️ F-I-M-A-P = "Finance ka MAP": File, Import, Match, Approve, Post.
  • 📄 H-I-T file: Header, Items, Trailer (check record). "File ne HIT kiya, total check karo".
  • 🌍 PK = Percentage Check: Pakistan needs VAT % + header check.
  • 5️⃣ "5 se shuru = e-invoice".
  • 🔁 Retrofit = "dono line barabar" (keep both lines equal).
  • 👤 CVI = "Customer Vendor Integration = BP ka dost" (keeps BP and vendor in sync).

🏢 Do these in your sandbox before interviews (then you can say "I did it")

  1. 📂 Write a small report that reads a test file from AL11 with OPEN DATASET (upload it with CG3Z) and checks a trailer total.
  2. 🧩 Call BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE for one 101 GR on test data + BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT; read the RETURN table (BDC tab).
  3. 🔌 Implement a BADI with only a breakpoint (e.g. MB_DOCUMENT_BADI) and see it stop in MIGO (BADI tab, L5).
  4. 🔹 Create one implicit enhancement in a Z FM's end (BADI tab, L7). Then delete it.
  5. ☁️ On BTP: CDS view + service binding preview (CDS + OData tabs).

⚡ Quick revise. Read top to bottom in 2 minutes before every interview.

🎤 Intro order

Who (ABAP developer & support consultant, 4.5 yrs, ECC on HANA, B2B wholesale, senior of 13, led 3–4) → What (F-I-M-A-P) → Build (e-invoice, Pakistan VAT) → Now (OOP, CDS, RAP, sandbox BADI / BAPI).

🗺️ F-I-M-A-P

  • F GR flat file (H-I-T) + XML invoice in AL11 · OPEN DATASET · CALL TRANSFORMATION.
  • I Y staging tables + status · vendor file → CVI vendor API → LFA1 / LFB1 · setup BP, FS00, FTXP.
  • M referencing = custom 3-way match (invoice no., order no., amount, supplier) · status machine.
  • A approval by status · P posting balances → posting file in AL11 → FI interfaces · debit / return notes.

⭐ Stories

  • 🌍 Unbalanced postings: only item sums checked → added header total + VAT % (tolerance) for PK → error status before FI. Better: config table.
  • 5️⃣ E-invoice: number starts with 5 → flag + new Y tables (provider ID, invoice ref, gov ID). Standard: DRC / EDOC_COCKPIT.
  • 📂 Support: wrong variant / path, incomplete file vs check record, text in number tag, missing mandatory country field → sender fixes, reprocess.
  • 🔁 Two DEV / test lines + one PRD → retrofit live changes.

🌉 Bridge

GR → BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE · invoice → BAPI_INCOMINGINVOICE_CREATE / MIRO (OMR6) · FI → BAPI_ACC_DOCUMENT_POST · files → IDoc / CPI · approval → workflow · custom code change vs BADI for standard.

🪤 Rules: say "did" + "practiced", never "only support". Don't claim BADI / OData / forms in the project. Education: "M.Tech", subject only if asked.
